# UGEKAZNA S5-reja: «Финмодель 2026–2032» + «AI-анализ» + «Экран» rejimi — implementatsiya rejasi

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** TZ 5-bosqichining qolgan qismi: 4 ssenariyli moliyaviy model (worker'da hisoblanadi, `mart`da saqlanadi, 4 sahifa + «Кредиты» DSCR), standart holatda o'chiq AI-анализ (server tomonida agregat kontekst, Claude API oqimi, limitlar, jurnal) va kiosk «Экран» rejimi.

**Architecture:** Sof hisob moduli `app/finmodel/model.py` (Decimal, DB'siz) 2-reja agregat freymvorkiga qo'shilgan `Aggregate.compute` bosqichida worker'da ishga tushadi: kirishlar витринадан (`app/finmodel/inputs.py`), допущения `core.fin_assumptions`dan, natija `mart|pub.agg_finmodel_year` (0020) ga; API (`/finmodel/model`) faqat 28 qatorni o'qib KPI/taqqoslash/sezgirlikni yig'adi. AI: `app/ai` — provayder protokoli (`ClaudeProvider` rasmiy `anthropic` SDK bilan, dev/test uchun `FakeProvider`), mavjud S1–S4 sahifa quruvchilari ustida oq ro'yxatli kontekst, PostgreSQL jurnalidagi limitlar (`core.ai_requests`, 0021), `POST /ai/analyze` SSE oqimi. Kiosk — frontend rejimi, uning so'rovlari `X-UGK-Background: 1` bilan idle muddatni uzaytirmaydi.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, FastAPI, SQLAlchemy 2 (sync) + psycopg 3, PostgreSQL 16, Redis 7, pydantic 2, APScheduler 3, `anthropic` (rasmiy Python SDK, async streaming), asyncio/anyio, pytest; React 18 + TypeScript strict, TanStack Query 5, Chart.js 4, Vitest + Testing Library + axe, Playwright 1.49.1.

**Spec:** [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-s5-finmodel-ai-design.md](../specs/2026-09-25-s5-finmodel-ai-design.md). Asos: 1-reja [2026-09-24-01-backend-yadro.md](2026-09-24-01-backend-yadro.md) (haqiqiy kod `backend/` da: `Ctx`, `AuthContext`, `build_context`, `require_perm`, `Section`, `section_perm`, `Perm`, ## Global Constraints

- 1–4-rejalar va S1–S4 rejalarining barcha Global Constraints'lari amal qiladi (Python `>=3.12`, hash bilan qulflangan bog'liqliklar, sirlar faqat Docker secrets, UTC, `/api/v1`, xato formati `{"error": {code, message, request_id, params?}}`, ruscha UI matnlari, o'zbekcha reja prozasi va izohlar).
- Backend testlari: `TEST="docker compose -f deploy/compose.test.yml run --rm test"`, keyin `$TEST pytest …` — haqiqiy PostgreSQL 16 va Redis; DB mock qilinmaydi. Frontend: `NODE="docker compose -f deploy/compose.frontend.yml run --rm node"`, keyin `$NODE npm …`; host Node ishlatilmaydi. Git Bash'da bind-mount'li `docker run` oldidan `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1`.
- Birliklar: pul — mln so'm (`numeric(20,2)`); quvvat — MVt; ishlab chiqarish va отпуск — mln kVt·soat (`numeric(20,3)`); tarif va tannarx — so'm/kVt·soat; ulushlar — 0..1; `date_id` = `YYYYMMDD`, `month_id` = `YYYYMM`.
- Финмодель formulalari — spec 3.1 aynan; model yillari `FIN_YEARS = 2026…2032`; ковенант faqat `core.settings` → `finmodel.dscr_covenant` (standart `1.2`); o'sish kalitlari (`tariff_growth`, `opex_index`) ikkinchi model yilidan qo'llanadi; skalyar допущения (`tariff_start`, `own_needs_share`, `capex_fx_share`) faqat `FIN_FIRST_YEAR = 2026` qatorida.
- Migratsiyalar: `0020_mart_finmodel` (`down_revision = "0019"`), `0021_ai_requests` (`"0020"`). `alembic heads` boshqa qiymat bersa — `down_revision` ni joriy head'ga moslang.
- UI faqat `pub.*` view'larni o'qiydi. `mart.agg_finmodel_year`: `build_id → core.builds ON DELETE CASCADE`, `branch_id = 0` (CHECK), `month_id` = build `as_of` oyi, `ENABLE` + `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`, policy `core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id)`; `pub.agg_finmodel_year` — `security_invoker = true`, aktiv build.
- Bo'lim endpointi: `GET`, `SQ`, router darajasida `require_perm(section_perm(Section.FINMODEL))`, `cache.respond(...)`; har yangi route `tests/test_route_guards.py` `EXPECTED_ROUTES` ga; `PREFIX_PERMS` ga `"/api/v1/finmodel": "section:finmodel:view"`.
- **AI standart holatda o'chiq:** `ai.enabled = false` (`core.settings`) yoki provayder sozlanmagan (`ai_api_key` bo'sh) — `/ai/analyze` → `404 ai_disabled`, UI tugmasi yashirin. Yoqish — faqat auditlanadigan sozlama o'zgarishi.
- **Haqiqiy Claude API hech qachon chaqirilmaydi** — testlarda, reja ijrosida va qo'lda tekshiruvda ham (tarmoqqa chiqish yo'q). Testlar provayderni `app.dependency_overrides[get_ai_provider]` yoki soxta SDK mijozi bilan almashtiradi; dev/E2E — `AI_PROVIDER=fake` (prod'da `problems()` rad etadi).
- Claude SDK: rasmiy `anthropic` paketi, `AsyncAnthropic` + `DefaultAsyncHttpxClient` (1.x SDK `httpx2` ustida — `httpx` obyektlari uzatilmaydi), `messages.stream(...)` / `beta.messages.stream(...)`, standart model `claude-opus-5` (`AI_MODEL` bilan o'zgaradi, sana qo'shimchasisiz), `thinking={"type": "adaptive"}`, `output_config={"effort": AI_EFFORT}`, `max_retries=0`; `temperature`, `budget_tokens`, assistant prefill yo'q.
- AI kontekst ≤ 14 000 belgi, faqat oq ro'yxatdagi agregat maydonlar; ФИО, pasport, INN, hisob raqami, kontragent/yetkazib beruvchi nomi, hujjat raqamlari yuborilmaydi; prompt, javob va savol matni log'ga va audit jurnaliga yozilmaydi (savol — faqat `core.ai_requests` da, 90 kun).
- AI limitlari: foydalanuvchiga `ai.user_per_hour` (20), tashkilotga `ai.org_per_day` (300); taym-aut 60 s (`AI_TIMEOUT_S`); jurnal saqlash 90 kun.
- AI kaliti — Docker secret `ai_api_key` (ixtiyoriy CA — `ai_ca_bundle`), faqat `api` servisiga va faqat `deploy/compose.ai.yml` overlay'i orqali; standart `compose.prod.yml` da `api` `egress` tarmog'ida emas.
- Kiosk: sessiya idle 30 daqiqa o'zgarmaydi; kiosk so'rovlari sarlavhasi `X-UGK-Background: 1` — server bunda `last_seen_at`/`idle_expires_at` ni yangilamaydi.
- Frontend: sahifalar `src/sections/finmodel/*.tsx`, yordamchilar `src/sections/finmodel/parts/`; AI — `src/ai/`, kiosk — `src/screen/`; barcha matnlar `t()` orqali (`src/i18n/ru.ts` oxiriga, `} as const satisfies …` dan oldin); CSP o'zgarmaydi (inline `<style>` yo'q). Yangi vizual naqsh yo'q — bloklar prototip `finmodel`, «AI-анализ» va `wall` qismlaridan (Refero kerak emas).
- Credential fayllari (`1C.1/secrets.php`, `auth.php`, `auth.js`, `apache-proxy.conf`, `gazna_bot/config.py`, har qanday `.env`, `deploy/secrets/`) ochilmaydi va hech qayerga ko'chirilmaydi.
- Commit xabari oxirida bo'sh qatordan keyin: `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Spec'dan ongli chetlanishlar yo'q; S5 qarorlari — spec 8-bo'lim.

## Review Focus

1. **Допущения build'dan keyin o'zgartirildi** — kutiladi: sahifa «допущения изменены после расчёта» deydi (eski raqamlarni yangi deb ko'rsatmaydi), «Пересчитать» (`rebuild`) yangi build'da yangi qiymatlarni beradi — 4-vazifa `test_assumption_change_applies_on_next_build` va `test_rebuild_source_publishes_new_model`, 5-vazifa `test_stale_assumptions_flag`, 13-vazifa `shows stale assumptions and input warnings`.
2. **Valyuta kreditining kursi yo'q yoki АИП rejasi kelgusi yillarga yuklanmagan** — kutiladi: kredit nolga aylantirilmaydi, `missing_fx` ro'yxatida; investitsiyasiz yillar UI'da «план АИП не загружен» — 3-vazifa `test_missing_fx_currency_excluded_and_listed`, 13-vazifa `shows stale assumptions and input warnings`.
3. **Финмодель ruxsati bor, lekin filial doirasi cheklangan rol** — kutiladi: 200 va `has_model = false` + tushuntirish (500 yoki bo'sh grafik emas) — 5-vazifa `test_branch_scoped_role_gets_no_model`, 13-vazifa `explains the company-level model when nothing is visible`.
4. **AI javobi kelayotganda drawer yopildi, tarmoq uzildi yoki jarayon to'xtadi** — kutiladi: jurnal yozuvi `cancelled`, `running` bo'lib qolib ketmaydi (10 daqiqadan keyin tozalanadi), limit hisobiga kiradi — 10-vazifa `test_client_disconnect_marks_cancelled`, 9-vazifa `test_purge_retention_and_stale_rows`, 15-vazifa `cancel aborts the stream and keeps the drawer usable`.
5. **Kontragent nomi va shartnoma raqamlari bor sahifa (Казначейство «Договоры и задолженность») tahlil qilinadi** — kutiladi: kontekstda kontragent nomi, raqam, INN yo'q; skaner topsa so'rov rad etiladi — 8-vazifa `test_kazna_dogovory_context_has_no_counterparty` va `test_every_page_context_is_clean`.

### Task 1: Финмодель допущения — yangi kalitlar, chegaralar, sozlamalar va `Aggregate.compute`

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/app/dwh/reference.py`, `backend/app/admin/dwh_schemas.py` (`FinAssumptionItem`), `backend/app/dwh/aggregates.py` (`Aggregate`, `build_all`), `backend/tests/test_reference.py`, `backend/tests/test_admin_dwh_api.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_s5_prereq.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SCENARIOS`, `FIN_YEARS`, `_BASE`, `DEFAULT_SETTINGS`, `SETTING_TYPES`, `validate_setting`, `Aggregate`, `BuildParams`, `build_all`, `builds.create_build`, `SCOPE_ALL_SQL`, `get_engine`.
- Produces:
  - `app.dwh.reference`: `FIN_YEAR_KEYS = ("tariff_growth", "generation_factor", "fx_rate_factor", "opex_index", "tax_rate", "kium", "capacity_add_mw")`, `FIN_SCALAR_KEYS = ("tariff_start", "own_needs_share", "capex_fx_share")`, `FIN_KEYS = FIN_YEAR_KEYS + FIN_SCALAR_KEYS`, `FIN_FIRST_YEAR = 2026`, `FIN_KEY_BOUNDS: dict[str, tuple[Decimal, Decimal]]`, `fin_value_error(year: int, key: str, value: Decimal) -> str | None` (`"key"`, `"year"`, `"value"` yoki `None`), `fin_assumption_seeds()` (4 × 7 × 7 yillik + 4 × 3 skalyar qator); sozlamalar `finmodel.dscr_covenant = 1.2` (float, `0 < x ≤ 5`), `finmodel.opex_exclude_items = []` (statya kodlari, ≤ 200, takrorsiz).
  - `app.admin.dwh_schemas.FinAssumptionItem` — `value` uchun umumiy ±10 o'rniga `fin_value_error` (422).
  - `app.dwh.aggregates`: `Compute = Callable[[Connection, Mapping[str, Any]], int]`, `Aggregate(table, statements, compute: Compute | None = None)`; `build_all` SQL'lardan keyin `compute(conn, bound)` ni chaqiradi va qator sonini qo'shadi.

- [ ] **Step 1: Testlarni yozish**

`backend/tests/test_s5_prereq.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Mapping
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.admin.dwh_schemas import FinAssumptionItem
from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.dwh import aggregates, builds
from app.dwh.aggregates import Aggregate, BuildParams
from app.dwh.reference import (
    DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
    FIN_FIRST_YEAR,
    FIN_KEY_BOUNDS,
    FIN_KEYS,
    FIN_SCALAR_KEYS,
    FIN_YEAR_KEYS,
    fin_assumption_seeds,
    fin_value_error,
    validate_setting,
)
from app.etl.db import SCOPE_ALL_SQL

NEW_KEYS = ("capacity_add_mw", "tariff_start", "own_needs_share", "capex_fx_share")


def test_keys_and_bounds() -> None:
    assert FIN_YEAR_KEYS[-1] == "capacity_add_mw"
    assert FIN_SCALAR_KEYS == ("tariff_start", "own_needs_share", "capex_fx_share")
    assert FIN_KEYS == FIN_YEAR_KEYS + FIN_SCALAR_KEYS and FIN_FIRST_YEAR == 2026
    assert set(FIN_KEY_BOUNDS) == set(FIN_KEYS)
    assert all(low < high for low, high in FIN_KEY_BOUNDS.values())


def test_seeds_scalar_keys_only_in_first_year() -> None:
    rows = fin_assumption_seeds()
    assert len(rows) == 4 * 7 * 7 + 4 * 3
    assert {r[1] for r in rows if r[2] in FIN_SCALAR_KEYS} == {2026}
    values = {(s, y, k): v for s, y, k, v in rows}
    assert values[("base", 2026, "capacity_add_mw")] == 0
    assert values[("base", 2027, "capacity_add_mw")] == Decimal(120)
    assert values[("fx", 2032, "capacity_add_mw")] == Decimal(30)
    assert values[("water", 2026, "tariff_start")] == Decimal(705)
    assert values[("base", 2026, "own_needs_share")] == Decimal("0.016")
    assert values[("tariff", 2026, "capex_fx_share")] == Decimal("0.4")
    assert values[("water", 2028, "generation_factor")] == Decimal("0.85")
    assert all(fin_value_error(y, k, v) is None for (_s, y, k), v in values.items())


def test_new_seed_rows_in_database() -> None:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        count = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM core.fin_assumptions WHERE is_current AND key = ANY(:k)"),
            {"k": list(NEW_KEYS)}).scalar_one()
    assert count == 4 * 7 + 4 * 3


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("year", "key", "value", "error"), [
    (2027, "tariff_start", Decimal(700), "year"),
    (2026, "tariff_start", Decimal(0), "value"),
    (2026, "kium", Decimal("1.5"), "value"),
    (2030, "capacity_add_mw", Decimal(-1), "value"),
    (2026, "magic", Decimal(1), "key"),
    (2026, "tariff_start", Decimal(705), None),
    (2031, "capacity_add_mw", Decimal(300), None),
    (2027, "fx_rate_factor", Decimal("1.2"), None),
])
def test_fin_value_error(year: int, key: str, value: Decimal, error: str | None) -> None:
    assert fin_value_error(year, key, value) == error


def test_admin_item_uses_key_bounds() -> None:
    item = FinAssumptionItem(scenario="base", year=2026, key="tariff_start", value=750)
    assert item.value == 750
    bad: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
        {"year": 2027, "key": "tariff_start", "value": 750},
        {"year": 2026, "key": "kium", "value": 2},
        {"year": 2026, "key": "magic", "value": 1},
        {"year": 2040, "key": "kium", "value": 0.4},
        {"year": 2027, "key": "capacity_add_mw", "value": float("nan")},
    ]
    for fields in bad:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            FinAssumptionItem(scenario="base", **fields)


def test_finmodel_settings() -> None:
    assert DEFAULT_SETTINGS["finmodel.dscr_covenant"] == 1.2
    assert DEFAULT_SETTINGS["finmodel.opex_exclude_items"] == []
    assert validate_setting("finmodel.dscr_covenant", 1.35) == 1.35
    assert validate_setting("finmodel.opex_exclude_items", ["1C-O-abc"]) == ["1C-O-abc"]
    bad: list[tuple[str, Any]] = [
        ("finmodel.dscr_covenant", 0), ("finmodel.dscr_covenant", "1.2"),
        ("finmodel.dscr_covenant", 6), ("finmodel.opex_exclude_items", [""]),
        ("finmodel.opex_exclude_items", ["A", "A"]),
        ("finmodel.opex_exclude_items", [f"X{i}" for i in range(201)]),
    ]
    for key, value in bad:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            validate_setting(key, value)


def test_aggregate_compute_default_is_none() -> None:
    assert Aggregate("agg_x", ("SELECT 1",)).compute is None


def test_build_all_calls_compute(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    seen: list[tuple[int, date]] = []

    def compute(conn: Connection, bound: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int:
        seen.append((int(bound["build_id"]), bound["as_of"]))
        return 3

    monkeypatch.setattr(aggregates, "AGGREGATES", [Aggregate("agg_cash_month", (), compute)])
    with get_engine("etl").connect() as conn:
        tx = conn.begin()
        try:
            conn.execute(text(SCOPE_ALL_SQL))
            build_id = builds.create_build(conn, None)
            out = aggregates.build_all(conn, build_id, BuildParams(
                as_of=date(2026, 9, 24), date_from=date(2025, 1, 1)))
        finally:
            tx.rollback()
    assert seen == [(build_id, date(2026, 9, 24))]
    assert out["agg_cash_month"] >= 3
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Допущения kalitlari, chegaralar va seed'lar**

`backend/app/dwh/reference.py` — `FIN_YEARS = range(2026, 2033)` qatoridan keyingi `FIN_KEYS = (…)`, `_BASE = {…}` va `fin_assumption_seeds()` ni (`ensure_reference` gacha) to'liq almashtiring:
```python
FIN_FIRST_YEAR = FIN_YEARS[0]
FIN_YEAR_KEYS = ("tariff_growth", "generation_factor", "fx_rate_factor", "opex_index", "tax_rate",
                 "kium", "capacity_add_mw")
# S5: skalyar допущения — faqat model birinchi yili (FIN_FIRST_YEAR) qatorida saqlanadi.
FIN_SCALAR_KEYS = ("tariff_start", "own_needs_share", "capex_fx_share")
FIN_KEYS = FIN_YEAR_KEYS + FIN_SCALAR_KEYS
# Har kalit uchun ruxsat etilgan oraliq (admin API tekshiradi; umumiy ±10 o'rniga).
FIN_KEY_BOUNDS: dict[str, tuple[Decimal, Decimal]] = {
    "tariff_growth": (Decimal("-0.5"), Decimal("1")),
    "generation_factor": (Decimal("0"), Decimal("2")),
    "fx_rate_factor": (Decimal("0.1"), Decimal("5")),
    "opex_index": (Decimal("-0.5"), Decimal("1")),
    "tax_rate": (Decimal("0"), Decimal("0.9")),
    "kium": (Decimal("0.01"), Decimal("1")),
    "capacity_add_mw": (Decimal("0"), Decimal("5000")),
    "tariff_start": (Decimal("1"), Decimal("100000")),
    "own_needs_share": (Decimal("0"), Decimal("0.5")),
    "capex_fx_share": (Decimal("0"), Decimal("1")),
}
# Bazaviy допущения — vaqtinchalik (TZ ochiq savoli), mijoz tasdiqlagach yangilanadi.
_BASE = {
    "tariff_growth": Decimal("0.08"),
    "generation_factor": Decimal("1"),
    "fx_rate_factor": Decimal("1"),
    "opex_index": Decimal("0.07"),
    "tax_rate": Decimal("0.15"),
    "kium": Decimal("0.40"),
    "tariff_start": Decimal("705"),  # prototip: 2026 tarifi, so'm/kVt·soat
    "own_needs_share": Decimal("0.016"),  # prototip ownSh
    "capex_fx_share": Decimal("0.4"),  # prototip: investitsiyalarning valyutali qismi
}
# Prototip addCap: АИП bo'yicha ввод мощностей, MVt (2026 — joriy yil, quvvat allaqachon hisobda).
_CAPACITY_ADD = {2026: 0, 2027: 120, 2028: 210, 2029: 95, 2030: 60, 2031: 40, 2032: 30}


def fin_value_error(year: int, key: str, value: Decimal) -> str | None:
    """Admin API tekshiruvi: noma'lum kalit, skalyar kalit noto'g'ri yilda, chegaradan tashqari."""
    if key not in FIN_KEY_BOUNDS:
        return "key"
    if key in FIN_SCALAR_KEYS and year != FIN_FIRST_YEAR:
        return "year"
    low, high = FIN_KEY_BOUNDS[key]
    if not value.is_finite() or not low <= value <= high:
        return "value"
    return None


def fin_assumption_seeds() -> list[tuple[str, int, str, Decimal]]:
    rows: list[tuple[str, int, str, Decimal]] = []
    for scenario in SCENARIOS:
        for year in FIN_YEARS:
            for key in FIN_YEAR_KEYS:
                value = Decimal(_CAPACITY_ADD[year]) if key == "capacity_add_mw" else _BASE[key]
                if scenario == "water" and key == "generation_factor" and 2027 <= year <= 2029:
                    value = Decimal("0.85")  # маловодье: выработка −15% в 2027–2029
                if scenario == "tariff" and key == "tariff_growth":
                    value = Decimal("0.04")  # рост тарифа 4% вместо 8%
                if scenario == "fx" and key == "fx_rate_factor":
                    value = Decimal("1.2")  # курс +20%
                rows.append((scenario, year, key, value))
            if year == FIN_FIRST_YEAR:
                rows.extend((scenario, year, key, _BASE[key]) for key in FIN_SCALAR_KEYS)
    return rows
```
`ensure_reference` o'zgarmaydi — yangi kalitlar keyingi bootstrap'da `WHERE NOT EXISTS` bilan qo'shiladi (mavjud qiymatlarga tegilmaydi).

```python
def _unique_codes(value: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    if len(set(value)) != len(value):
        raise ValueError("takrorlangan statya kodi")
    return value
```
`DEFAULT_SETTINGS` lug'ati oxiriga qo'shing:

- [ ] **Step 5: Admin API — har kalit chegarasi**

`backend/app/admin/dwh_schemas.py` — `app.dwh.reference` importidagi `FIN_KEYS` ni `fin_value_error` ga almashtiring (`FIN_YEARS` qoladi), `from decimal import Decimal` va pydantic importiga `model_validator` ni qo'shing; `FinAssumptionItem` ni to'liq almashtiring:
```python
class FinAssumptionItem(_In):
    scenario: Literal["base", "water", "tariff", "fx"]
    year: int
    key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=64)
    value: float = Field(allow_inf_nan=False)

    @field_validator("year")
    @classmethod
    def _year(cls, value: int) -> int:
        if value not in FIN_YEARS:
            raise ValueError("year")
        return value

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _bounds(self) -> "FinAssumptionItem":
        # S5: har kalitning o'z oralig'i (tarif — so'mda, quvvat — MVt), skalyar — faqat 2026.
        error = fin_value_error(self.year, self.key, Decimal(str(self.value)))
        if error is not None:
            raise ValueError(error)
        return self
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `Aggregate.compute`**

`backend/app/dwh/aggregates.py` — importlarga `from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping` ni qo'shing; `Aggregate` dataclass'ini almashtiring:
```python
Compute = Callable[[Connection, Mapping[str, Any]], int]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Aggregate:
    table: str  # mart sxemasidagi jadval
    statements: tuple[str, ...]
    # S5: Python bosqichi (masalan, финмодель) — SQL'lardan keyin, o'sha tranzaksiyada chaqiriladi,
    # yozilgan qatorlar sonini qaytaradi.
    compute: Compute | None = None
```
`build_all` ichidagi `for aggregate in AGGREGATES:` sikli tanasini almashtiring:
```python
    for aggregate in AGGREGATES:
        rows = conn.execute(text(_CARRY_FORWARD.format(table=aggregate.table)), bound).rowcount
        rows += sum(conn.execute(text(sql), bound).rowcount for sql in aggregate.statements)
        if aggregate.compute is not None:
            rows += aggregate.compute(conn, bound)
        out[aggregate.table] = rows
```

### Task 2: Sof финмодель — TZ formulalari, xulosa, taqqoslash va sezgirlik

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:
  - `app.finmodel.model`: `ZERO`, `ONE`, `YearAssumptions(tariff_growth, generation_factor, fx_rate_factor, opex_index, tax_rate, kium, capacity_add_mw)`, `ScalarAssumptions(tariff_start, own_needs_share, capex_fx_share)`, `ScenarioAssumptions(years: Mapping[int, YearAssumptions], scalars)`, `DebtYear(uzs_principal=0, uzs_interest=0, fx_principal=0, fx_interest=0)` (valyuta qismi — so'mda, `as_of` kursida), `NO_DEBT`, `FinInputs(capacity_mw, opex_base, opening_cash, aip_plan: Mapping[int, Decimal], debt: Mapping[int, DebtYear])`, `YearRow(year, capacity_mw, generation, supply, tariff, revenue, opex, tax, ebitda, margin, capex_plan, capex, principal, interest, debt_service, net_flow, balance, dscr, covenant_breach, unit_cost)` (hammasi `Decimal`, `margin`/`dscr`/`unit_cost` — `Decimal | None`), `year_hours(year) -> Decimal`, `run_model(inputs, scenario, *, covenant: Decimal) -> list[YearRow]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Testlarni yozish**

`backend/tests/test_finmodel_model.py`:
```python
from decimal import Decimal

import pytest

from app.finmodel.model import (
    DebtYear,
    FinInputs,
    ScalarAssumptions,
    ScenarioAssumptions,
    YearAssumptions,
    run_model,
    year_hours,
)
from app.finmodel.summary import from_row, sensitivity, summarize

D = Decimal
COVENANT = D("1.2")
INPUTS = FinInputs(
    capacity_mw=D(100), opex_base=D(100000), opening_cash=D(500),
    aip_plan={2026: D(30000), 2027: D(20000)},
    debt={2026: DebtYear(D(20000), D(10000), D(8000), D(2000)),
          2027: DebtYear(D(20000), D(8000), D(8000), D(1500))})
SCALARS = ScalarAssumptions(tariff_start=D(500), own_needs_share=D("0.02"),
                            capex_fx_share=D("0.4"))


def _year(**over: Decimal) -> YearAssumptions:
    base = {"tariff_growth": D("0.08"), "generation_factor": D(1), "fx_rate_factor": D(1),
            "opex_index": D("0.10"), "tax_rate": D("0.1"), "kium": D("0.5"),
            "capacity_add_mw": D(0)}
    base.update(over)
    return YearAssumptions(**base)


def scenario(name: str) -> ScenarioAssumptions:
    years: dict[int, YearAssumptions] = {}
    for year in (2026, 2027, 2028):
        over: dict[str, Decimal] = {}
        if year == 2027:
            over["capacity_add_mw"] = D(20)
        if name == "water" and year == 2027:
            over["generation_factor"] = D("0.85")
        if name == "tariff":
            over["tariff_growth"] = D("0.04")
        if name == "fx":
            over["fx_rate_factor"] = D("1.2")
        years[year] = _year(**over)
    return ScenarioAssumptions(years, SCALARS)


def q(value: Decimal | None, places: str = "0.0001") -> Decimal | None:
    return None if value is None else value.quantize(D(places))


def test_year_hours_leap() -> None:
    assert year_hours(2026) == 8760 and year_hours(2028) == 8784


def test_base_scenario_by_hand() -> None:
    y26, y27, y28 = run_model(INPUTS, scenario("base"), covenant=COVENANT)
    assert (y26.capacity_mw, y26.generation, y26.supply, y26.tariff) == (
        100, D("438"), D("429.24"), 500)
    assert (y26.revenue, y26.tax, y26.opex, y26.ebitda) == (
        D("214620"), D("21462"), D(100000), D("93158"))
    assert (y26.capex_plan, y26.capex, y26.principal, y26.interest, y26.debt_service) == (
        30000, 30000, 28000, 12000, 40000)
    assert (y26.net_flow, y26.balance, y26.dscr) == (D("23158"), D("23658"), D("2.32895"))
    assert q(y26.margin) == D("0.4341") and q(y26.unit_cost, "0.01") == D("282.97")
    assert not y26.covenant_breach
    assert (y27.capacity_mw, y27.generation, y27.tariff, y27.opex) == (
        120, D("525.6"), D("540"), D("110000"))
    assert (y27.revenue, y27.ebitda, y27.debt_service, y27.net_flow, y27.balance) == (
        D("278147.52"), D("140332.768"), 37500, D("82832.768"), D("106490.768"))
    assert q(y27.dscr) == D("3.7422")
    assert (y28.generation, y28.tariff, y28.opex) == (D("527.04"), D("583.2"), D("121000"))
    assert (y28.revenue, y28.ebitda, y28.capex, y28.debt_service) == (
        D("301222.33344"), D("150100.100096"), 0, 0)
    assert y28.dscr is None and not y28.covenant_breach
    assert y28.balance == D("256590.868096")


def test_water_tariff_fx_scenarios_by_hand() -> None:
    water = run_model(INPUTS, scenario("water"), covenant=COVENANT)
    assert (water[1].generation, water[1].revenue, water[1].ebitda) == (
        D("446.76"), D("236425.392"), D("102782.8528"))
    assert q(water[1].dscr) == D("2.7409") and water[1].balance == D("68940.8528")
    assert water[2].generation == D("527.04")  # маловодье faqat berilgan yillarda
    tariff = run_model(INPUTS, scenario("tariff"), covenant=COVENANT)
    assert (tariff[1].tariff, tariff[1].revenue, tariff[2].tariff) == (
        D("520"), D("267845.76"), D("540.8"))
    fx = run_model(INPUTS, scenario("fx"), covenant=COVENANT)
    assert (fx[0].capex, fx[0].principal, fx[0].interest, fx[0].debt_service) == (
        D("32400"), D("29600"), D("12400"), D("42000"))
    assert (fx[0].net_flow, fx[0].balance) == (D("18758"), D("19258"))
    assert q(fx[0].dscr) == D("2.2180")
    assert fx[0].ebitda == D("93158")  # kurs EBITDA'ga ta'sir qilmaydi (TZ formulasi)


def test_covenant_flag_uses_given_threshold() -> None:
    rows = run_model(INPUTS, scenario("base"), covenant=D("2.5"))
    assert [r.covenant_breach for r in rows] == [True, False, False]


def test_zero_capacity_has_no_division_by_zero() -> None:
    empty = FinInputs(capacity_mw=D(0), opex_base=D(1000), opening_cash=D(0), aip_plan={},
                      debt={2026: DebtYear(uzs_principal=D(100))})
    years = {2026: _year()}
    (row,) = run_model(empty, ScenarioAssumptions(years, SCALARS), covenant=COVENANT)
    assert row.revenue == 0 and row.margin is None and row.unit_cost is None
    assert row.ebitda == D(-1000) and row.dscr == D(-10) and row.covenant_breach
    assert row.balance == D(-1100)


def test_summary_and_verdict() -> None:
    rows = [from_row(r) for r in run_model(INPUTS, scenario("base"), covenant=COVENANT)]
    s = summarize("base", rows, base_capacity=100.0, covenant=1.2)
    assert (s.capacity_last, s.capacity_added, s.revenue_first) == (120.0, 20.0, 214620.0)
    assert s.revenue_last == pytest.approx(301222.33344)
    assert s.ebitda_total == pytest.approx(383590.868096)
    assert s.margin_avg == pytest.approx(0.4789633202)
    assert (s.capex_total, s.debt_service_total) == (50000.0, 77500.0)
    assert (s.dscr_min, s.dscr_min_year) == (pytest.approx(2.32895), 2026)
    assert s.cum_flow == pytest.approx(256090.868096)
    assert (s.negative_years, s.verdict) == (0, "funded")
    strict = summarize("base", rows, base_capacity=100.0, covenant=2.5)
    assert strict.verdict == "needs_financing"


def test_negative_balance_needs_financing() -> None:
    heavy = FinInputs(capacity_mw=INPUTS.capacity_mw, opex_base=INPUTS.opex_base,
                      opening_cash=INPUTS.opening_cash, aip_plan={2026: D(500000)},
                      debt=INPUTS.debt)
    rows = [from_row(r) for r in run_model(heavy, scenario("base"), covenant=COVENANT)]
    s = summarize("base", rows, base_capacity=100.0, covenant=1.2)
    assert rows[0].balance == pytest.approx(-446342.0)
    assert s.negative_years >= 1 and s.cum_flow < 0 and s.verdict == "needs_financing"


def test_sensitivity_against_base() -> None:
    summaries = {
        name: summarize(name, [from_row(r) for r in run_model(
            INPUTS, scenario(name), covenant=COVENANT)], base_capacity=100.0, covenant=1.2)
        for name in ("base", "water", "tariff", "fx")}
    out = sensitivity(summaries)
    assert [x.scenario for x in out] == ["water", "tariff", "fx"]
    assert out[0].delta_cum == pytest.approx(-37549.9152)
    assert out[1].delta_cum == pytest.approx(-28981.193472)
    assert out[2].delta_cum == pytest.approx(-7900.0)
    assert out[2].dscr_min == pytest.approx(2.2180476)
    assert sensitivity({k: v for k, v in summaries.items() if k != "base"}) == []
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Sof model**

`backend/app/finmodel/__init__.py`:
```python
"""Финансовая модель 2026–2032 (TZ 4, S5 spec 3): sof hisob, kirishlar, xulosa."""
```

`backend/app/finmodel/model.py`:
```python
"""Финмодель 2026–2032 — sof hisob (TZ 4 «Финансовая модель», S5 spec 3.1).

DB, vaqt va tarmoqqa bog'liq emas: kirish — витрина faktlaridan yig'ilgan `FinInputs` va
bitta ssenariy допущения'lari (`ScenarioAssumptions`), chiqish — yillar bo'yicha `YearRow`.
Hisob Decimal'da; yaxlitlash faqat saqlashda (mart ustunlari) bajariladi.

Birliklar: pul — mln so'm; quvvat — MVt; ishlab chiqarish/отпуск — mln kVt·soat; tarif va
tannarx — so'm/kVt·soat; ulushlar — 0..1.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import calendar
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

ZERO = Decimal(0)
ONE = Decimal(1)
THOUSAND = Decimal(1000)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class YearAssumptions:
    tariff_growth: Decimal  # tarif o'sishi (ikkinchi model yilidan)
    generation_factor: Decimal  # ishlab chiqarish koeffitsienti (маловодье: 0,85)
    fx_rate_factor: Decimal  # kurs koeffitsienti (ослабление сума: 1,2)
    opex_index: Decimal  # operatsion xarajatlar o'sishi (ikkinchi model yilidan)
    tax_rate: Decimal  # soliqlar va majburiy to'lovlar — tushumdan ulush
    kium: Decimal  # КИУМ
    capacity_add_mw: Decimal  # АИП bo'yicha yil ichida ishga tushadigan quvvat


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ScalarAssumptions:
    tariff_start: Decimal  # birinchi model yilining tarifi, so'm/kVt·soat
    own_needs_share: Decimal  # o'z ehtiyojlari ulushi (отпуск = выработка × (1 − ulush))
    capex_fx_share: Decimal  # investitsiyalarning valyutali qismi (kurs tuzatmasi uchun)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ScenarioAssumptions:
    years: Mapping[int, YearAssumptions]
    scalars: ScalarAssumptions


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DebtYear:
    """Kredit grafigi bir yil uchun, so'mda (valyuta qismi — `as_of` kursida)."""

    uzs_principal: Decimal = ZERO
    uzs_interest: Decimal = ZERO
    fx_principal: Decimal = ZERO
    fx_interest: Decimal = ZERO


NO_DEBT = DebtYear()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class FinInputs:
    """Витрина faktlari (build sanasi `as_of` holatiga)."""

    capacity_mw: Decimal  # o'rnatilgan quvvat (stansiyalar yig'indisi)
    opex_base: Decimal  # operatsion xarajatlar: oxirgi 12 yopilgan oy (soliq/kreditsiz)
    opening_cash: Decimal  # hisoblardagi qoldiq
    aip_plan: Mapping[int, Decimal]  # АИП rejasi yillar bo'yicha
    debt: Mapping[int, DebtYear]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class YearRow:
    year: int
    capacity_mw: Decimal
    generation: Decimal
    supply: Decimal
    tariff: Decimal
    revenue: Decimal
    opex: Decimal
    tax: Decimal
    ebitda: Decimal
    margin: Decimal | None
    capex_plan: Decimal
    capex: Decimal
    principal: Decimal
    interest: Decimal
    debt_service: Decimal
    net_flow: Decimal
    balance: Decimal
    dscr: Decimal | None
    covenant_breach: bool
    unit_cost: Decimal | None


def year_hours(year: int) -> Decimal:
    """«Часы года» — kalendar soatlari (kabisa yili 8784)."""
    return Decimal((366 if calendar.isleap(year) else 365) * 24)


def run_model(inputs: FinInputs, scenario: ScenarioAssumptions, *,
              covenant: Decimal) -> list[YearRow]:
    """TZ 4 formulalari; yillar — `scenario.years` kalitlari o'sish tartibida."""
    rows: list[YearRow] = []
    s = scenario.scalars
    capacity = inputs.capacity_mw
    balance = inputs.opening_cash
    tariff = s.tariff_start
    opex = inputs.opex_base
    for index, year in enumerate(sorted(scenario.years)):
        a = scenario.years[year]
        capacity += a.capacity_add_mw  # мощность года = предыдущего + ввод по АИП
        if index:
            tariff *= ONE + a.tariff_growth
            opex *= ONE + a.opex_index  # база текущего года × индекс роста
        generation = capacity * year_hours(year) * a.kium * a.generation_factor / THOUSAND
        supply = generation * (ONE - s.own_needs_share)
        revenue = supply * tariff
        tax = revenue * a.tax_rate
        ebitda = revenue - opex - tax
        capex_plan = inputs.aip_plan.get(year, ZERO)
        capex = capex_plan * (ONE + (a.fx_rate_factor - ONE) * s.capex_fx_share)
        debt = inputs.debt.get(year, NO_DEBT)
        principal = debt.uzs_principal + debt.fx_principal * a.fx_rate_factor
        interest = debt.uzs_interest + debt.fx_interest * a.fx_rate_factor
        service = principal + interest
        net = ebitda - capex - service
        balance += net
        dscr = ebitda / service if service > ZERO else None
        rows.append(YearRow(
            year=year, capacity_mw=capacity, generation=generation, supply=supply,
            tariff=tariff, revenue=revenue, opex=opex, tax=tax, ebitda=ebitda,
            margin=ebitda / revenue if revenue else None, capex_plan=capex_plan, capex=capex,
            principal=principal, interest=interest, debt_service=service, net_flow=net,
            balance=balance, dscr=dscr, covenant_breach=dscr is not None and dscr < covenant,
            unit_cost=(opex + tax) / supply if supply > ZERO else None))
    return rows
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Xulosa va sezgirlik**

`backend/app/finmodel/summary.py`:
```python
"""Ssenariy xulosasi, taqqoslash va sezgirlik — saqlangan yillik qatorlardan (so'rovda, arzon).

Kirish — `pub.agg_finmodel_year` qatorlari (float). Prototip `fin()`/`scenTable()` mantiqi:
«требует привлечения финансирования» — minimal DSCR ковенантдан past yoki jamg'arilgan oqim manfiy.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Literal

from app.finmodel.model import YearRow

Verdict = Literal["funded", "needs_financing"]
BASE = "base"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class FinYear:
    year: int
    capacity_mw: float
    generation: float
    supply: float
    tariff: float
    revenue: float
    opex: float
    tax: float
    ebitda: float
    margin: float | None
    capex_plan: float
    capex: float
    principal: float
    interest: float
    debt_service: float
    net_flow: float
    balance: float
    dscr: float | None
    covenant_breach: bool
    unit_cost: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ScenarioSummary:
    scenario: str
    capacity_last: float
    capacity_added: float
    revenue_first: float
    revenue_last: float
    ebitda_total: float
    margin_avg: float | None
    capex_total: float
    debt_service_total: float
    dscr_min: float | None
    dscr_min_year: int | None
    cum_flow: float
    negative_years: int
    verdict: Verdict


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Sensitivity:
    scenario: str
    delta_cum: float
    dscr_min: float | None


def _opt(value: Decimal | None) -> float | None:
    return None if value is None else float(value)


def from_row(row: YearRow) -> FinYear:
    """Sof model natijasini float qatorga (testlar va saqlash tekshiruvi uchun)."""
    return FinYear(
        year=row.year, capacity_mw=float(row.capacity_mw), generation=float(row.generation),
        supply=float(row.supply), tariff=float(row.tariff), revenue=float(row.revenue),
        opex=float(row.opex), tax=float(row.tax), ebitda=float(row.ebitda),
        margin=_opt(row.margin), capex_plan=float(row.capex_plan), capex=float(row.capex),
        principal=float(row.principal), interest=float(row.interest),
        debt_service=float(row.debt_service), net_flow=float(row.net_flow),
        balance=float(row.balance), dscr=_opt(row.dscr), covenant_breach=row.covenant_breach,
        unit_cost=_opt(row.unit_cost))


def summarize(scenario: str, years: Sequence[FinYear], *, base_capacity: float,
              covenant: float) -> ScenarioSummary:
    if not years:
        raise ValueError("bo'sh ssenariy")
    ordered = sorted(years, key=lambda y: y.year)
    margins = [y.margin for y in ordered if y.margin is not None]
    with_dscr = [(y.dscr, y.year) for y in ordered if y.dscr is not None]
    worst = min(with_dscr) if with_dscr else None
    cum = sum(y.net_flow for y in ordered)
    dscr_min = None if worst is None else worst[0]
    bad = (dscr_min is not None and dscr_min < covenant) or cum < 0
    return ScenarioSummary(
        scenario=scenario, capacity_last=ordered[-1].capacity_mw,
        capacity_added=ordered[-1].capacity_mw - base_capacity,
        revenue_first=ordered[0].revenue, revenue_last=ordered[-1].revenue,
        ebitda_total=sum(y.ebitda for y in ordered),
        margin_avg=sum(margins) / len(margins) if margins else None,
        capex_total=sum(y.capex for y in ordered),
        debt_service_total=sum(y.debt_service for y in ordered),
        dscr_min=dscr_min, dscr_min_year=None if worst is None else worst[1],
        cum_flow=cum, negative_years=sum(1 for y in ordered if y.balance < 0),
        verdict="needs_financing" if bad else "funded")


def sensitivity(summaries: Mapping[str, ScenarioSummary]) -> list[Sensitivity]:
    """Jamg'arilgan oqimning bazaviy ssenariyga nisbatan o'zgarishi — kattasi birinchi."""
    base = summaries.get(BASE)
    if base is None:
        return []
    out = [Sensitivity(s.scenario, s.cum_flow - base.cum_flow, s.dscr_min)
           for s in summaries.values() if s.scenario != BASE]
    return sorted(out, key=lambda x: (-abs(x.delta_cum), x.scenario))
```

### Task 3: Финмодель kirishlari — витрина faktlari, допущения va АИП kelgusi yillari (sintetik)

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/finmodel/inputs.py`, `backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/outlook.py`, `backend/tests/s5_helpers.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/__init__.py` (`FAMILIES`)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_finmodel_inputs.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `FinInputs`, `DebtYear`, `YearAssumptions`, `ScalarAssumptions`, `ScenarioAssumptions`, `ZERO`, `FIN_YEARS`, `FIN_FIRST_YEAR`, `FIN_YEAR_KEYS`, `FIN_SCALAR_KEYS`, `SCENARIOS`, `current_setting` (`kazna.calendar_kinds`, `finmodel.opex_exclude_items`, `finmodel.dscr_covenant`), `dwh.cashflow_effective` (S2), `dwh.dim_branch_version`, `dwh.fact_balance`, `dwh.fact_investment`, `dwh.fact_loan_schedule`, `dwh.dim_loan`, `dwh.fx_rate`, `add_months`, `month_start`, `date_id`, `etl_transaction`, `tests.etl_helpers.*`, `tests.s1_helpers.rebuild`, `tests.s2_helpers.put_setting`, sintetik `SynthModel`, `money`, `OBJECTS`, `BRANCHES`, `FUNDS`, `investment.annual_plan`.
- Produces:
  - `app.finmodel.inputs`: `InputsMeta(as_of: date, opex_months: int, missing_fx: tuple[str, ...])`, `Assumptions(scenarios: dict[str, ScenarioAssumptions], updated_at: datetime | None, covenant: Decimal)`, `opex_window(as_of) -> tuple[date, date]` (oxirgi 12 yopilgan oy), `opex_excluded_items(conn) -> list[str]`, `gather_inputs(conn, as_of) -> tuple[FinInputs, InputsMeta]`, `load_assumptions(conn) -> Assumptions` (yo'q допущение → `ValueError`).
  - `app.etl.synthetic.families.outlook`: `OUTLOOK_DOC = "aip_outlook"`, `SHARES = (0.8, 0.6, 0.4)`, `generate(model, window, sink) -> {"fact_investment": n}` — har obyekt uchun keyingi 3 yil (≤ 2032) rejasi yil 1-yanvari qatorida.
  - `tests.s5_helpers`: `FX = "XFM"`, `NO_RATE = "XFN"`, `FinScenario(source, tag, run_id, build_id, items)`, `finmodel_rows(tag) -> dict[str, list[dict]]`, `finmodel_scenario(source, *, publish=False) -> FinScenario`, `put_fin(scenario, year, key, value) -> None` (versiyali yozuv, `update_fin_assumptions` kabi), `fin_value(scenario, year, key) -> Decimal`.

Ssenariy qatorlari (`finmodel_rows`, as_of = 2026-09-24, xarajat oynasi 2025-09-01…2026-08-31):
| Fakt | Qatorlar | Kirishga ta'siri |
|---|---|---|
| filial `T-<tag>` | `ges`, quvvat 120,5 | quvvat +120,5 |
| rasxod | `OP` (operating) 1200 (2026-03-15) + 100 (2025-09-15); 700 (2025-08-31, oynadan oldin); 400 (2026-09-10, ochiq oy); `EX` (operating, `finmodel.opex_exclude_items`) 300; `IV` (investing) 500; `TX` (operating, `kazna.calendar_kinds.taxes`) 250; `OP` kirim 999 | xarajat bazasi +1300 |
| qoldiq | hisob `ACC-<tag>`: 650 (2026-08-31), 800 (2026-09-20) | qoldiq +800 |
| АИП | obyekt `OF-<tag>`: ochilish (2025-01-01, reja 0), 100 (2026-03-01), 200 (2026-10-01), 500 (2027-01-01), 999 (2033-01-01) | 2026 → +300, 2027 → +500 |
| kreditlar | `LU` (UZS): 100 + 30 (2026-10-10), 300 + 18 (2027-10-10); `LF` (`XFM`, kurs 1000 → 1100 (2026-09-01) → 1300 (2026-10-01, as_of dan keyin)): 1 + 0,5 (2026-09-15), 2 + 0,4 (2027-03-15); `LN` (`XFN`, kurs yo'q): 5 + 1 (2026-12-01) | 2026: so'm 100/30, valyuta 1100/550; 2027: so'm 300/18, valyuta 2200/440; `missing_fx` ∋ `XFN` |

`backend/tests/s5_helpers.py`:
```python
"""S5 test yordamchilari: финмодель kirishlari ssenariysi va допущения'ni vaqtincha o'zgartirish."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.db import get_engine
from tests.etl_helpers import branch_row, cash_row, merge_all, new_tag, open_run, stage
from tests.s1_helpers import rebuild

FX, NO_RATE = "XFM", "XFN"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class FinScenario:
    source: str
    tag: str
    run_id: int
    build_id: int
    items: dict[str, str]  # rol → statya kodi: op, ex, iv, tx


def finmodel_rows(tag: str) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    branch = branch_row(tag, capacity_mw=120.5, kind="ges")
    code = branch["code"]
    op, ex, iv, tx = f"OP-{tag}", f"EX-{tag}", f"IV-{tag}", f"TX-{tag}"
    bank = f"ACC-{tag}"
    src, obj = f"SF-{tag}", f"OF-{tag}"
    lu, lf, ln = f"LU-{tag}", f"LF-{tag}", f"LN-{tag}"

    def item(item_code: str, activity: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"code": item_code, "name": f"Статья {item_code}", "activity": activity,
                "direction": "out", "sort_order": 900}

    def inv(src_id: str, day: int, plan: float, fact: float = 0,
            doc: str = "aip_month") -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"doc_type": doc, "src_id": src_id, "date_id": day, "branch_code": code,
                "object_code": obj, "source_code": src, "plan": plan, "fact": fact,
                "physical_pct": None, "advance": 0, "advance_date_id": None}

    def loan(loan_code: str, currency: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"code": loan_code, "creditor": f"Тест кредитор {loan_code}",
                "project": "Финмодель тест", "currency": currency, "rate": 0.05,
                "grace_until": None, "maturity": "2030-12-31", "amount_uzs": 1000,
                "branch_code": code}

    def sched(src_id: str, loan_code: str, day: int, principal: float,
              interest: float) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"doc_type": "loan_schedule", "src_id": src_id, "date_id": day,
                "branch_code": code, "loan_code": loan_code, "principal": principal,
                "interest": interest, "balance": 0}

    return {
        "dim_branch": [branch],
        "dim_item": [item(op, "operating"), item(ex, "operating"), item(iv, "investing"),
                     item(tx, "operating")],
        "dim_bank": [{"code": bank, "bank_name": "Тест банк", "currency": "UZS"}],
        "dim_source": [{"code": src, "name": f"Бюджет {tag}", "kind": "budget"}],
        "dim_object": [{"code": obj, "name": f"Объект {tag}", "program_code": "АИП-Ф-1",
                        "work_type": "new", "commissioning_year": 2028, "total_cost": 5000,
                        "source_code": src}],
        "dim_loan": [loan(lu, "UZS"), loan(lf, FX), loan(ln, NO_RATE)],
        "fx_rate": [{"currency": FX, "date_id": 20260801, "rate": 1000},
                    {"currency": FX, "date_id": 20260901, "rate": 1100},
                    {"currency": FX, "date_id": 20261001, "rate": 1300}],
        "fact_cashflow": [
            cash_row("c1", 20260315, code, 1200, item_code=op),
            cash_row("c2", 20250915, code, 100, item_code=op),
            cash_row("c3", 20250831, code, 700, item_code=op),
            cash_row("c4", 20260910, code, 400, item_code=op),
            cash_row("c5", 20260401, code, 300, item_code=ex),
            cash_row("c6", 20260402, code, 500, item_code=iv),
            cash_row("c7", 20260403, code, 250, item_code=tx),
            cash_row("c8", 20260404, code, 999, item_code=op, direction="in"),
        ],
        "fact_balance": [
            {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "b1", "date_id": 20260831, "branch_code": code,
             "bank_code": bank, "balance": 650},
            {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "b2", "date_id": 20260920, "branch_code": code,
             "bank_code": bank, "balance": 800},
        ],
        "fact_investment": [
            inv("i0", 20250101, 0, 300, doc="aip_opening"), inv("i1", 20260301, 100),
            inv("i2", 20261001, 200), inv("i3", 20270101, 500), inv("i4", 20330101, 999),
        ],
        "fact_loan_schedule": [
            sched("u1", lu, 20261010, 100, 30), sched("u2", lu, 20271010, 300, 18),
            sched("f1", lf, 20260915, 1, 0.5), sched("f2", lf, 20270315, 2, 0.4),
            sched("n1", ln, 20261201, 5, 1),
        ],
    }


def finmodel_scenario(source: str, *, publish: bool = False) -> FinScenario:
    tag = new_tag()
    run_id = open_run(source)
    stage(run_id, source, finmodel_rows(tag))
    merge_all(run_id, source)
    build_id = rebuild(run_id, publish=publish)
    items = {"op": f"OP-{tag}", "ex": f"EX-{tag}", "iv": f"IV-{tag}", "tx": f"TX-{tag}"}
    return FinScenario(source, tag, run_id, build_id, items)


def fin_value(scenario: str, year: int, key: str) -> Decimal:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        return Decimal(conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT value FROM core.fin_assumptions WHERE scenario = :s AND year = :y "
            "AND key = :k AND is_current"), {"s": scenario, "y": year, "k": key}).scalar_one())


def put_fin(scenario: str, year: int, key: str, value: Decimal) -> None:
    """core.fin_assumptions ga yangi versiya (admin `update_fin_assumptions` bilan bir xil)."""
    params = {"s": scenario, "y": year, "k": key}
    with get_engine("app").begin() as conn:
        version = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM core.fin_assumptions WHERE scenario = :s AND year = :y "
            "AND key = :k"), params).scalar_one()
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.fin_assumptions SET is_current = false WHERE scenario = :s "
                          "AND year = :y AND key = :k AND is_current"), params)
        conn.execute(text(
            "INSERT INTO core.fin_assumptions (scenario, year, key, version, value) "
            "VALUES (:s, :y, :k, :v, :val)"), {**params, "v": version + 1, "val": value})
```

`backend/tests/test_finmodel_inputs.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.dwh.reference import current_setting
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.synthetic.families import outlook
from app.etl.synthetic.families.investment import annual_plan
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import OBJECTS
from app.finmodel.inputs import (
    InputsMeta,
    gather_inputs,
    load_assumptions,
    opex_excluded_items,
    opex_window,
)
from app.finmodel.model import DebtYear, FinInputs
from tests.etl_helpers import (
    FULL_WINDOW,
    TEST_AS_OF,
    merge_all,
    new_source,
    new_tag,
    open_run,
    purge_source,
    stage,
)
from tests.s2_helpers import put_setting
from tests.s5_helpers import NO_RATE, finmodel_rows, finmodel_scenario


def _gather() -> tuple[FinInputs, InputsMeta]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        return gather_inputs(conn, TEST_AS_OF)


def _debt(inputs: FinInputs, year: int) -> tuple[Decimal, ...]:
    d = inputs.debt.get(year, DebtYear())
    return d.uzs_principal, d.uzs_interest, d.fx_principal, d.fx_interest


def _delta(after: FinInputs, before: FinInputs, year: int) -> list[Decimal]:
    return [a - b for a, b in zip(_debt(after, year), _debt(before, year), strict=True)]


@pytest.fixture
def excluded_items() -> Iterator[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Ssenariyning EX va TX statyalarini sozlamalar orqali xarajat bazasidan chiqaradi."""
    tag = new_tag()
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        kinds = current_setting(conn, "kazna.calendar_kinds")
        excluded = current_setting(conn, "finmodel.opex_exclude_items")
    put_setting("kazna.calendar_kinds", {**kinds, "taxes": [*kinds.get("taxes", []),
                                                           f"TX-{tag}"]})
    put_setting("finmodel.opex_exclude_items", [*excluded, f"EX-{tag}"])
    yield tag, f"EX-{tag}"
    put_setting("kazna.calendar_kinds", kinds)
    put_setting("finmodel.opex_exclude_items", excluded)


def test_opex_window_is_last_twelve_closed_months() -> None:
    assert opex_window(date(2026, 9, 24)) == (date(2025, 9, 1), date(2026, 8, 31))
    assert opex_window(date(2026, 1, 1)) == (date(2025, 1, 1), date(2025, 12, 31))


def test_inputs_delta_from_scenario(excluded_items: tuple[str, str]) -> None:
    """Delta usuli: boshqa ma'lumot bo'lsa ham faqat ssenariy hissasi tekshiriladi."""
    tag, _ = excluded_items
    before, _ = _gather()
    source = new_source()
    try:
        run_id = open_run(source)
        stage(run_id, source, finmodel_rows(tag))
        merge_all(run_id, source)
        after, meta = _gather()
    finally:
        purge_source(source)
    assert after.capacity_mw - before.capacity_mw == Decimal("120.5")
    assert after.opex_base - before.opex_base == Decimal(1300)
    assert after.opening_cash - before.opening_cash == Decimal(800)
    for year, delta in ((2026, 300), (2027, 500), (2028, 0), (2032, 0)):
        got = after.aip_plan.get(year, Decimal(0)) - before.aip_plan.get(year, Decimal(0))
        assert got == Decimal(delta), year
    assert 2033 not in after.aip_plan
    assert _delta(after, before, 2026) == [Decimal(100), Decimal(30), Decimal(1100),
                                           Decimal(550)]
    assert _delta(after, before, 2027) == [Decimal(300), Decimal(18), Decimal(2200),
                                           Decimal(440)]
    assert meta.as_of == TEST_AS_OF and 1 <= meta.opex_months <= 12


def test_missing_fx_currency_excluded_and_listed() -> None:
    source = new_source()
    try:
        finmodel_scenario(source)
        _, meta = _gather()
    finally:
        purge_source(source)
    assert NO_RATE in meta.missing_fx
    _, cleaned = _gather()
    assert NO_RATE not in cleaned.missing_fx


def test_opex_excluded_items_merge_settings(excluded_items: tuple[str, str]) -> None:
    tag, ex = excluded_items
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        codes = opex_excluded_items(conn)
    assert f"TX-{tag}" in codes and ex in codes and codes == sorted(set(codes))


def test_load_assumptions_four_scenarios() -> None:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        a = load_assumptions(conn)
    assert set(a.scenarios) == {"base", "water", "tariff", "fx"}
    assert a.scenarios["water"].years[2028].generation_factor == Decimal("0.85")
    assert a.scenarios["tariff"].years[2030].tariff_growth == Decimal("0.04")
    assert a.scenarios["base"].years[2027].capacity_add_mw == Decimal(120)
    assert a.scenarios["fx"].scalars.tariff_start == Decimal(705)
    assert a.covenant == Decimal("1.2") and a.updated_at is not None


def test_load_assumptions_missing_row_fails() -> None:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        tx = conn.begin()
        try:
            conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.fin_assumptions SET is_current = false "
                              "WHERE scenario = 'fx' AND year = 2030 AND key = 'kium'"))
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="fx/2030/kium"):
                load_assumptions(conn)
        finally:
            tx.rollback()


class _Sink:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.rows: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []

    def add(self, target: str, row: Any) -> None:
        self.rows.append((target, dict(row)))


def test_outlook_family_rows() -> None:
    sink = _Sink()
    counts = outlook.generate(SynthModel(916202617, TEST_AS_OF), FULL_WINDOW, sink)
    rows = [r for t, r in sink.rows if t == "fact_investment"]
    assert counts == {"fact_investment": len(OBJECTS) * 3} == {"fact_investment": len(rows)}
    assert {r["date_id"] for r in rows} == {20270101, 20280101, 20290101}
    first = [r for r in rows if r["object_code"] == "OBJ-01"]
    assert [float(r["plan"]) for r in first] == pytest.approx(
        [annual_plan(1, 2026) * s for s in outlook.SHARES], rel=1e-4)
    assert all(r["doc_type"] == outlook.OUTLOOK_DOC and r["fact"] == 0
               and r["physical_pct"] is None for r in rows)
    late = _Sink()
    outlook.generate(SynthModel(916202617, date(2031, 3, 1)), FULL_WINDOW, late)
    assert {r["date_id"] // 10000 for _, r in late.rows} <= {2032}
```
(`test_missing_fx_currency_excluded_and_listed` oxiridagi ikkinchi `_gather()` — ssenariy tozalangach `XFN` ro'yxatdan yo'qolishini tekshiradi. `excluded_items` fixture'i sozlamalarni ssenariy yuklanishidan OLDIN o'zgartiradi — «oldin» va «keyin» bir xil istisnolar bilan hisoblanadi.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — kirishlar va допущения**

`backend/app/finmodel/inputs.py`:
```python
"""Финмодель kirishlari витринадан (worker, ugk_etl, scope_all) va допущения jadvalidan.

Faqat build `as_of` sanasigacha ma'lum bo'lgan faktlar (S5 spec 3.3):
- quvvat — `dim_branch_version` (`as_of` da amalda), stansiyalar (`ges`, `cascade`);
- xarajat bazasi — oxirgi 12 YOPILGAN oy operatsion rasxodi (`dwh.cashflow_effective`, S2
  ustuvorligi bilan), soliq/kredit statyalari (`kazna.calendar_kinds`) va
  `finmodel.opex_exclude_items` siz; yillikka keltirilmaydi (qator soni `opex_months`);
- boshlang'ich qoldiq — har hisobning `as_of` gacha oxirgi `fact_balance` qiymati yig'indisi;
- АИП rejasi — `fact_investment.plan` yillar bo'yicha (forward qatorlar);
- kredit grafigi — yillar bo'yicha; valyuta — `as_of` gacha oxirgi ЦБ kursi, kursi yo'q valyuta
  hisobga olinmaydi va `missing_fx` ga yoziladi (S2 qarori).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.dwh.dates import add_months, date_id, month_start
from app.dwh.reference import (
    FIN_FIRST_YEAR,
    FIN_SCALAR_KEYS,
    FIN_YEAR_KEYS,
    FIN_YEARS,
    SCENARIOS,
    current_setting,
)
from app.finmodel.model import (
    ZERO,
    DebtYear,
    FinInputs,
    ScalarAssumptions,
    ScenarioAssumptions,
    YearAssumptions,
)

_CAPACITY = """
SELECT coalesce(sum(v.capacity_mw), 0)
FROM dwh.dim_branch b
JOIN dwh.dim_branch_version v ON v.branch_id = b.branch_id
 AND CAST(:as_of AS date) BETWEEN v.valid_from AND coalesce(v.valid_to, DATE '9999-12-31')
WHERE b.kind IN ('ges', 'cascade')
"""

_OPEX = """
SELECT coalesce(sum(f.amount), 0) AS amount, count(DISTINCT d.month_id) AS months
FROM dwh.cashflow_effective f
JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = f.date_id
JOIN dwh.dim_item i ON i.item_id = f.item_id
WHERE f.direction = 'out' AND i.activity = 'operating'
  AND NOT (i.code = ANY(CAST(:excluded AS text[])))
  AND f.date_id BETWEEN :from_id AND :to_id
"""

_CASH = """
SELECT coalesce(sum(z.balance), 0) FROM (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (fb.branch_id, fb.bank_id) fb.balance
  FROM dwh.fact_balance fb
  WHERE fb.date_id <= :as_of_id
  ORDER BY fb.branch_id, fb.bank_id, fb.date_id DESC) z
"""

_AIP = """
SELECT d.year, sum(i.plan) AS plan
FROM dwh.fact_investment i
JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = i.date_id
WHERE d.year BETWEEN :y0 AND :y1
GROUP BY d.year
"""

_DEBT = """
WITH rates AS (
  SELECT DISTINCT ON (currency) currency, rate
  FROM dwh.fx_rate WHERE date_id <= :as_of_id
  ORDER BY currency, date_id DESC)
SELECT d.year, l.currency, sum(s.principal) AS principal, sum(s.interest) AS interest,
       max(r.rate) AS rate
FROM dwh.fact_loan_schedule s
JOIN dwh.dim_loan l ON l.loan_id = s.loan_id
JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = s.date_id
LEFT JOIN rates r ON r.currency = l.currency
WHERE d.year BETWEEN :y0 AND :y1
GROUP BY d.year, l.currency
"""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class InputsMeta:
    as_of: date
    opex_months: int  # xarajat bazasida ma'lumoti bor oylar (12 dan kam — UI ogohlantiradi)
    missing_fx: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Assumptions:
    scenarios: dict[str, ScenarioAssumptions]
    updated_at: datetime | None  # joriy допущения'ning eng so'nggi o'zgarishi
    covenant: Decimal


def opex_window(as_of: date) -> tuple[date, date]:
    first = month_start(as_of)
    return add_months(first, -12), first - timedelta(days=1)


def opex_excluded_items(conn: Connection) -> list[str]:
    kinds = current_setting(conn, "kazna.calendar_kinds")
    extra = current_setting(conn, "finmodel.opex_exclude_items")
    return sorted({*kinds.get("taxes", []), *kinds.get("loans", []), *extra})


def gather_inputs(conn: Connection, as_of: date) -> tuple[FinInputs, InputsMeta]:
    years = {"y0": FIN_YEARS[0], "y1": FIN_YEARS[-1]}
    as_of_id = date_id(as_of)
    capacity = Decimal(conn.execute(text(_CAPACITY), {"as_of": as_of}).scalar_one())
    start, end = opex_window(as_of)
    opex = conn.execute(text(_OPEX), {"excluded": opex_excluded_items(conn),
                                      "from_id": date_id(start), "to_id": date_id(end)}).one()
    cash = Decimal(conn.execute(text(_CASH), {"as_of_id": as_of_id}).scalar_one())
    aip = {int(r.year): Decimal(r.plan) for r in conn.execute(text(_AIP), years)}
    parts: dict[int, list[Decimal]] = {}
    missing: set[str] = set()
    for r in conn.execute(text(_DEBT), {**years, "as_of_id": as_of_id}):
        acc = parts.setdefault(int(r.year), [ZERO, ZERO, ZERO, ZERO])
        if r.currency == "UZS":
            acc[0] += r.principal
            acc[1] += r.interest
        elif r.rate is None:
            missing.add(str(r.currency))
        else:
            acc[2] += r.principal * r.rate
            acc[3] += r.interest * r.rate
    debt = {year: DebtYear(*acc) for year, acc in parts.items()}
    inputs = FinInputs(capacity_mw=capacity, opex_base=Decimal(opex.amount), opening_cash=cash,
                       aip_plan=aip, debt=debt)
    return inputs, InputsMeta(as_of, int(opex.months), tuple(sorted(missing)))


def _need(values: dict[tuple[str, int, str], Decimal], scenario: str, year: int,
          key: str) -> Decimal:
    try:
        return values[(scenario, year, key)]
    except KeyError:
        raise ValueError(f"финмодель: допущение {scenario}/{year}/{key} yo'q") from None


def load_assumptions(conn: Connection) -> Assumptions:
    rows = conn.execute(text(
        "SELECT scenario, year, key, value, updated_at FROM core.fin_assumptions "
        "WHERE is_current")).all()
    values = {(str(r.scenario), int(r.year), str(r.key)): Decimal(r.value) for r in rows}
    scenarios: dict[str, ScenarioAssumptions] = {}
    for scenario in SCENARIOS:
        years = {year: YearAssumptions(**{k: _need(values, scenario, year, k)
                                          for k in FIN_YEAR_KEYS}) for year in FIN_YEARS}
        scalars = ScalarAssumptions(**{k: _need(values, scenario, FIN_FIRST_YEAR, k)
                                       for k in FIN_SCALAR_KEYS})
        scenarios[scenario] = ScenarioAssumptions(years, scalars)
    covenant = Decimal(str(current_setting(conn, "finmodel.dscr_covenant")))
    updated = max((r.updated_at for r in rows), default=None)
    return Assumptions(scenarios, updated, covenant)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implementatsiya — АИП kelgusi yillari (sintetik)**

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/outlook.py`:
```python
"""fact_investment — АИП rejasining kelgusi yillari (dev/test sintetikasi, S5 финмодель uchun).

S4 `investment` oilasi rejani faqat joriy yil oxirigacha beradi; финмодель 2027+ yillar
investitsiyalarini ham talab qiladi. Har obyekt uchun keyingi 3 yil (2032 dan oshmay) rejasi —
joriy yil rejasining 0,8 / 0,6 / 0,4 ulushi, yil 1-yanvari qatorida (fakt 0). Deterministik.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

from app.dwh.dates import date_id
from app.etl.synthetic.families.investment import annual_plan
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel, money
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import BRANCHES, FUNDS, OBJECTS
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window

OUTLOOK_DOC = "aip_outlook"
SHARES = (0.8, 0.6, 0.4)
LAST_YEAR = 2032


def generate(model: SynthModel, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> dict[str, int]:
    count = 0
    base_year = model.as_of.year
    for n, (_name, b, _kind, src, _total, _plan, _deadline) in enumerate(OBJECTS, start=1):
        for step, share in enumerate(SHARES, start=1):
            year = base_year + step
            if year > LAST_YEAR:
                break
            day = date(year, 1, 1)
            if day < window.date_from:
                continue
            sink.add("fact_investment", {
                "doc_type": OUTLOOK_DOC, "src_id": f"OUT-{n:02d}-{year}",
                "date_id": date_id(day), "branch_code": BRANCHES[b].code,
                "object_code": f"OBJ-{n:02d}", "source_code": FUNDS[src][0],
                "plan": money(annual_plan(n, base_year) * share), "fact": money(0),
                "physical_pct": None, "advance": money(0), "advance_date_id": None})
            count += 1
    return {"fact_investment": count}
```

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/__init__.py` — import ro'yxatiga `outlook` ni (`investment` dan keyin) qo'shing va `FAMILIES` oxiriga:
```python

```
(`tests/test_synthetic.py` yoki `tests/test_synthetic_s4.py` `fact_investment` qatorlari sonini aniq tekshirsa — oilalar bo'yicha yig'indiga `len(OBJECTS) * 3` ni qo'shing.)

- [ ] **Step 1: Testlarni yozish**

`backend/tests/test_finmodel_aggregates.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.db import apply_rls, new_session
from app.dwh import builds
from app.etl.connectors import KNOWN_SOURCES, make_connector
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.notify import LogNotifier
from app.etl.rebuild import RebuildConnector
from app.etl.runner import Runner
from app.etl.types import Mode
from app.finmodel.inputs import gather_inputs, load_assumptions
from app.finmodel.model import run_model
from tests.etl_helpers import FULL_WINDOW, TEST_AS_OF, new_source, purge_source
from tests.s1_helpers import rebuild
from tests.s5_helpers import FinScenario, fin_value, finmodel_scenario, put_fin

COLS = ("revenue", "opex", "tax", "ebitda", "capex", "debt_service", "net_flow", "balance")


def _q(value: Decimal, places: str) -> Decimal:
    return value.quantize(Decimal(places), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)


@pytest.fixture
def sc() -> Iterator[FinScenario]:
    source = new_source()
    yield finmodel_scenario(source)
    purge_source(source)


def _rows(build_id: int) -> dict[tuple[str, int], dict[str, Any]]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT * FROM mart.agg_finmodel_year WHERE build_id = :b"), {"b": build_id}
        ).mappings().all()
    return {(r["scenario"], int(r["year"])): dict(r) for r in rows}


def test_build_stores_four_scenarios_by_seven_years(sc: FinScenario) -> None:
    rows = _rows(sc.build_id)
    assert len(rows) == 28
    assert {s for s, _ in rows} == {"base", "water", "tariff", "fx"}
    assert {y for _, y in rows} == set(range(2026, 2033))
    first = rows[("base", 2026)]
    assert (first["branch_id"], first["month_id"], first["as_of_id"]) == (0, 202609, 20260924)
    assert first["covenant"] == Decimal("1.2") and "XFN" in first["missing_fx"]
    assert first["assumptions_at"] is not None and 1 <= first["opex_months"] <= 12


def test_rows_match_pure_model(sc: FinScenario) -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        inputs, _ = gather_inputs(conn, TEST_AS_OF)
        assumptions = load_assumptions(conn)
    stored = _rows(sc.build_id)
    for scenario, sa in assumptions.scenarios.items():
        for row in run_model(inputs, sa, covenant=assumptions.covenant):
            got = stored[(scenario, row.year)]
            for col in COLS:
                expected = _q(getattr(row, col), "0.01")
                assert got[col] == expected, (scenario, row.year, col)
            assert got["generation"] == _q(row.generation, "0.001")
            assert got["covenant_breach"] is row.covenant_breach
            if row.dscr is None:
                assert got["dscr"] is None
            else:
                assert got["dscr"] == _q(row.dscr, "0.0001")
    water, base = stored[("water", 2028)], stored[("base", 2028)]
    assert abs(water["generation"] - base["generation"] * Decimal("0.85")) <= Decimal("0.001")


def test_rls_only_scope_all(sc: FinScenario) -> None:
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, frozenset({1, 3}))
        scoped = s.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM mart.agg_finmodel_year "
                                "WHERE build_id = :b"), {"b": sc.build_id}).scalar_one()
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, None)
        full = s.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM mart.agg_finmodel_year "
                              "WHERE build_id = :b"), {"b": sc.build_id}).scalar_one()
    assert (scoped, full) == (0, 28)


def test_failed_build_removes_rows(sc: FinScenario) -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        builds.mark_failed(conn, sc.build_id)
    assert _rows(sc.build_id) == {}


def test_assumption_change_applies_on_next_build(sc: FinScenario) -> None:
    before = _rows(sc.build_id)[("tariff", 2027)]["revenue"]
    original = fin_value("tariff", 2027, "tariff_growth")
    put_fin("tariff", 2027, "tariff_growth", Decimal("0.2"))
    try:
        after = _rows(rebuild(sc.run_id))[("tariff", 2027)]["revenue"]
    finally:
        put_fin("tariff", 2027, "tariff_growth", original)
    assert after > before
    assert _rows(sc.build_id)[("tariff", 2027)]["revenue"] == before  # eski build o'zgarmaydi


def test_rebuild_connector_extracts_nothing() -> None:
    connector = make_connector("rebuild")
    assert isinstance(connector, RebuildConnector) and "rebuild" in KNOWN_SOURCES
    assert connector.healthcheck().ok and connector.watermark().value is None
    assert connector.control_totals(FULL_WINDOW) is None
    stats = connector.extract(FULL_WINDOW, _NoSink())
    assert (stats.rows, dict(stats.by_target)) == (0, {})


class _NoSink:
    def add(self, target: str, row: Any) -> None:
        raise AssertionError("rebuild hech narsa yozmasligi kerak")


def test_rebuild_source_publishes_new_model(published_synthetic: Any) -> None:
    original = fin_value("base", 2030, "kium")
    put_fin("base", 2030, "kium", Decimal("0.45"))
    try:
        result = Runner(make_connector("rebuild"), mode=Mode.FULL, notifier=LogNotifier(),
                        today=TEST_AS_OF).run()
    finally:
        put_fin("base", 2030, "kium", original)
    assert result.status == "success", result.error
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, None)
        active = s.execute(text("SELECT active_build_id FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1")
                           ).scalar_one()
        count = s.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM pub.agg_finmodel_year")).scalar_one()
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        run = conn.execute(text("SELECT source, rows_in FROM core.etl_run WHERE id = :r"),
                           {"r": result.run_id}).one()
    assert active == result.build_id and count == 28
    assert (run.source, run.rows_in) == ("rebuild", 0)
```
(`published_synthetic` — 2-reja sessiya fixture'i; `rebuild` yugurishi faqat agregatlarni qayta quradi va publish qiladi — boshqa testlar kutgan faktlar o'zgarmaydi.)

- [ ] **Step 3: 0020 migratsiyasi**

`backend/migrations/versions/0020_mart_finmodel.py`:
```python
"""S5: финмодель — 4 ssenariy × 7 yil, build bo'yicha (kirish bazasi har qatorda)

Revision ID: 0020
Revises: 0019
Create Date: 2026-09-25

Faqat kompaniya darajasi: branch_id = 0 — RLS bo'yicha faqat scope_all foydalanuvchilar ko'radi.
"""

from alembic import op

revision = "0020"
down_revision = "0019"  # `alembic heads` boshqa bo'lsa — shu qiymatni joriy head'ga moslang
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None

MONEY = "numeric(20,2) NOT NULL"
ACTIVE = "(SELECT d.active_build_id FROM core.data_version d WHERE d.id = 1)"
COLUMNS = (
    "branch_id, month_id, scenario, year, capacity_mw, generation, supply, tariff, revenue, opex, "
    "tax, ebitda, margin, capex_plan, capex, principal, interest, debt_service, net_flow, "
    "balance, dscr, covenant_breach, unit_cost, as_of_id, base_capacity_mw, opex_base, "
    "opex_months, opening_cash, missing_fx, assumptions_at, covenant"
)

STATEMENTS = [
    f"""
    CREATE TABLE mart.agg_finmodel_year (
      build_id bigint NOT NULL REFERENCES core.builds (build_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      branch_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (branch_id = 0),
      month_id integer NOT NULL,
      scenario varchar(8) NOT NULL CHECK (scenario IN ('base', 'water', 'tariff', 'fx')),
      year smallint NOT NULL CHECK (year BETWEEN 2020 AND 2100),
      capacity_mw numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
      generation numeric(20,3) NOT NULL,
      supply numeric(20,3) NOT NULL,
      tariff numeric(14,2) NOT NULL,
      revenue {MONEY},
      opex {MONEY},
      tax {MONEY},
      ebitda {MONEY},
      margin numeric(12,6),
      capex_plan {MONEY},
      capex {MONEY},
      principal {MONEY},
      interest {MONEY},
      debt_service {MONEY},
      net_flow {MONEY},
      balance {MONEY},
      dscr numeric(18,4),
      covenant_breach boolean NOT NULL,
      unit_cost numeric(14,2),
      as_of_id integer NOT NULL,
      base_capacity_mw numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
      opex_base {MONEY},
      opex_months smallint NOT NULL,
      opening_cash {MONEY},
      missing_fx varchar(8)[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{{}}',
      assumptions_at timestamptz,
      covenant numeric(6,3) NOT NULL,
      PRIMARY KEY (build_id, branch_id, month_id, scenario, year)
    )
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE VIEW pub.agg_finmodel_year WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT {COLUMNS}
    FROM mart.agg_finmodel_year a WHERE a.build_id = {ACTIVE}
    """,
    "ALTER TABLE mart.agg_finmodel_year ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY",
    "ALTER TABLE mart.agg_finmodel_year FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY",
    "CREATE POLICY branch_scope ON mart.agg_finmodel_year USING (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id)) "
    "WITH CHECK (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id))",
    "GRANT SELECT ON mart.agg_finmodel_year TO ugk_app",
    "GRANT SELECT, INSERT, DELETE ON mart.agg_finmodel_year TO ugk_etl",
    "GRANT SELECT ON pub.agg_finmodel_year TO ugk_app",
]


def upgrade() -> None:
    for statement in STATEMENTS:
        op.execute(statement)


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.execute("DROP VIEW pub.agg_finmodel_year")
    op.execute("DROP TABLE mart.agg_finmodel_year")
```
(`'{{}}'` — f-satr ichida bo'sh massiv literal `'{}'`.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Agregat bosqichi**

`backend/app/dwh/finmodel_aggregates.py`:
```python
"""S5 финмодель agregati — Python bosqichi (`Aggregate.compute`, spec 3.4).

Build tranzaksiyasida (ugk_etl, scope_all): витрина kirishlari + joriy допущения → sof model
(`app.finmodel.model`) → 4 ssenariy × 7 yil. Qiymatlar mart ustunlari aniqligigacha yaxlitlanadi;
kirish bazasi (quvvat, xarajat bazasi, qoldiq, kursi yo'q valyutalar, допущения vaqti, ковенант)
har qatorga yoziladi — UI «Исходные данные модели» va «допущения изменены» belgisi uchun.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.dwh.aggregates import Aggregate, register
from app.dwh.dates import date_id, month_id
from app.finmodel.inputs import gather_inputs, load_assumptions
from app.finmodel.model import YearRow, run_model

_INSERT = """
INSERT INTO mart.agg_finmodel_year (
  build_id, branch_id, month_id, scenario, year, capacity_mw, generation, supply, tariff,
  revenue, opex, tax, ebitda, margin, capex_plan, capex, principal, interest, debt_service,
  net_flow, balance, dscr, covenant_breach, unit_cost, as_of_id, base_capacity_mw, opex_base,
  opex_months, opening_cash, missing_fx, assumptions_at, covenant)
VALUES (
  :build_id, 0, :month_id, :scenario, :year, :capacity_mw, :generation, :supply, :tariff,
  :revenue, :opex, :tax, :ebitda, :margin, :capex_plan, :capex, :principal, :interest,
  :debt_service, :net_flow, :balance, :dscr, :covenant_breach, :unit_cost, :as_of_id,
  :base_capacity_mw, :opex_base, :opex_months, :opening_cash, :missing_fx, :assumptions_at,
  :covenant)
"""
_MONEY = ("revenue", "opex", "tax", "ebitda", "capex_plan", "capex", "principal", "interest",
          "debt_service", "net_flow", "balance")


def _q(value: Decimal, places: str) -> Decimal:
    return value.quantize(Decimal(places), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)


def _opt(value: Decimal | None, places: str) -> Decimal | None:
    return None if value is None else _q(value, places)


def _row(row: YearRow) -> dict[str, Any]:
    out: dict[str, Any] = {col: _q(getattr(row, col), "0.01") for col in _MONEY}
    out.update(
        year=row.year, capacity_mw=_q(row.capacity_mw, "0.01"),
        generation=_q(row.generation, "0.001"), supply=_q(row.supply, "0.001"),
        tariff=_q(row.tariff, "0.01"), margin=_opt(row.margin, "0.000001"),
        dscr=_opt(row.dscr, "0.0001"), covenant_breach=row.covenant_breach,
        unit_cost=_opt(row.unit_cost, "0.01"))
    return out


def compute_finmodel(conn: Connection, bound: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int:
    as_of = bound["as_of"]
    inputs, meta = gather_inputs(conn, as_of)
    assumptions = load_assumptions(conn)
    base = {
        "build_id": bound["build_id"], "month_id": month_id(as_of), "as_of_id": date_id(as_of),
        "base_capacity_mw": _q(inputs.capacity_mw, "0.01"),
        "opex_base": _q(inputs.opex_base, "0.01"), "opex_months": meta.opex_months,
        "opening_cash": _q(inputs.opening_cash, "0.01"), "missing_fx": list(meta.missing_fx),
        "assumptions_at": assumptions.updated_at, "covenant": assumptions.covenant,
    }
    rows = [{**base, "scenario": scenario, **_row(r)}
            for scenario, sa in assumptions.scenarios.items()
            for r in run_model(inputs, sa, covenant=assumptions.covenant)]
    conn.execute(text(_INSERT), rows)
    return len(rows)


FINMODEL_YEAR = register(Aggregate("agg_finmodel_year", (), compute_finmodel))
```

```python
"""Manbasiz qayta hisob (S5 spec 3.4): faktlar o'zgarmaydi — Runner agregatlarni (финмодель
ham), sifat tekshiruvlarini va publish'ni bajaradi. Admin «Пересчитать модель» →

from __future__ import annotations

from app.etl.types import (
    ControlTotals,
    ExtractStats,
    HealthResult,
    StagingSink,
    Watermark,
    Window,
)


class RebuildConnector:
    source = "rebuild"

    def healthcheck(self) -> HealthResult:
        return HealthResult(True)

    def watermark(self) -> Watermark:
        return Watermark(None)

    def extract(self, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> ExtractStats:
        return ExtractStats(0, {})  # hech bir maqsad «to'liq» emas — hech narsa o'chirilmaydi

    def control_totals(self, window: Window) -> ControlTotals | None:
        return None
```

`backend/tests/s5_helpers.py` — importlarga `from app.auth.models import Role, RolePermission` va `from app.core.db import new_session` ni qo'shing (`get_engine` importi yoniga), fayl oxiriga:
```python
def scoped_role(code: str, perms: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
    """Filial doirasi cheklangan (scope_all = false) sinov roli."""
    with new_session() as s:
        role = Role(code=code, name=f"Тест {code}", scope_all=False, is_system=False)
        s.add(role)
        s.flush()
        for perm in perms:
            s.add(RolePermission(role_id=role.id, permission_code=perm))
        s.commit()
    return code
```
