```python
    client = make_client(None, create_user("rukovodstvo"))
    assert client.get("/api/v1/ai/status").json()["reason"] == "not_configured"
    assert client.post(URL, json={"section": "obzor", "page": "svod"}).status_code == 404


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("published_synthetic", "enabled")
def test_stream_events_journal_and_audit(make_client: Any) -> None:
    provider = FakeProvider(["## Что важно заметить\n", "- Остаток **1,2 трлн сум**\n"])
    user = create_user("rukovodstvo")
    client = make_client(provider, user)
    r = client.post(URL, json={"section": "obzor", "page": "svod", "per": "ytd"})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert r.headers["content-type"].startswith("text/event-stream")
    assert r.headers["cache-control"] == "no-store"
    got = events(r.text)
    assert [e for e, _ in got] == ["start", "delta", "delta", "done"]
    request_id = got[0][1]["request_id"]
    assert got[-1][1] == {"length": len("## Что важно заметить\n- Остаток **1,2 трлн сум**\n")}
    (row,) = _journal()
    assert (row["id"], row["status"], row["user_id"], row["section"]) == (
        request_id, "ok", user.id, "obzor")
    assert row["answer_chars"] == got[-1][1]["length"] and row["context_chars"] > 0
    assert row["filters"] == {"br": None, "per": "ytd"} and row["finished_at"] is not None
    system, prompt = provider.calls[0]
    assert "## Что важно заметить" in system
    assert prompt.startswith("<данные>\n") and "Проанализируй показатели страницы." in prompt
    audit = _audit("ai.request")[-1]
    assert (audit["result"], audit["object_id"]) == ("ok", str(request_id))
    assert audit["details"]["section"] == "obzor" and "question" not in audit["details"]
    assert client.get("/api/v1/ai/status").json()["used_last_hour"] == 1


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("published_synthetic", "enabled")
def test_question_preset_and_validation(make_client: Any) -> None:
    provider = FakeProvider(["ok"])
    client = make_client(provider, create_user("rukovodstvo"))
    body = {"section": "kredity", "page": "svod", "question": "Почему вырос долг?"}
    assert client.post(URL, json=body).status_code == 200
    assert "<вопрос>\nПочему вырос долг?\n</вопрос>" in provider.calls[-1][1]
    assert _journal()[-1]["question"] == "Почему вырос долг?"
    assert client.post(URL, json={"section": "kredity", "page": "svod",
                                  "preset": "risks"}).status_code == 200
    assert "Какие главные риски?" in provider.calls[-1][1]
    both = client.post(URL, json={**body, "preset": "risks"})
    assert both.status_code == 422
    inn = client.post(URL, json={**body, "question": "Кто владелец ИНН 301234567?"})
    assert (inn.status_code, inn.json()["error"]["code"]) == (422, "ai_question_sensitive")
    assert len(_journal()) == 2


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("published_synthetic", "enabled")
def test_forbidden_section_scope_and_unknown_page(make_client: Any) -> None:

                            create_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3,)))

@pytest.mark.usefixtures("published_synthetic", "enabled")
def test_rate_limit_user(make_client: Any) -> None:
    put_setting("ai.user_per_hour", 1)
    client = make_client(FakeProvider(["x"]), create_user("rukovodstvo"))
    assert client.post(URL, json={"section": "obzor", "page": "svod"}).status_code == 200
    r = client.post(URL, json={"section": "obzor", "page": "svod"})
    assert r.status_code == 429
    error = r.json()["error"]
    assert error["code"] == "ai_limit" and error["params"]["scope"] == "user"
    assert int(r.headers["Retry-After"]) == error["params"]["retry_after"] > 3500
    assert _audit("ai.request")[-1]["result"] == "denied"


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("published_synthetic", "enabled")
def test_provider_error_event(make_client: Any) -> None:
    provider = FakeProvider(["Частичный ответ"], error="ai_refused", error_after=1)
    client = make_client(provider, create_user("rukovodstvo"))
    got = events(client.post(URL, json={"section": "obzor", "page": "svod"}).text)
    assert [e for e, _ in got] == ["start", "delta", "error"]
    assert got[-1][1]["code"] == "ai_refused" and got[-1][1]["message"]
    assert (_journal()[-1]["status"], _journal()[-1]["error_code"]) == ("refused", "ai_refused")


def _job(provider: FakeProvider) -> Job:
    user = create_user("rukovodstvo")
    request_id = journal.reserve(journal.NewRequest(
        user.id, "obzor", "svod", {}, None, None, 10, provider.name, provider.model),
        now=datetime.now(UTC))
    return Job(request_id, "SYS", "PROMPT", provider)


@pytest.mark.anyio
async def test_timeout_closes_provider_and_journals() -> None:
    provider = FakeProvider(["a", "b"], delay=0.5)
    job = _job(provider)
    frames = [f async for f in stream_events(job, timeout=0.1)]
    got = events(b"".join(frames).decode())
    assert [e for e, _ in got] == ["start", "error"] and got[-1][1]["code"] == "ai_timeout"
    await asyncio.sleep(0)
    assert provider.closed == 1
    row = _journal()[-1]
    assert (row["status"], row["error_code"]) == ("timeout", "ai_timeout")


@pytest.mark.anyio
async def test_client_disconnect_marks_cancelled() -> None:
    provider = FakeProvider(["a", "b", "c"], delay=0.01)
    job = _job(provider)
    stream = stream_events(job)
    first = [await anext(stream), await anext(stream)]  # start + birinchi delta
    await stream.aclose()  # brauzer AbortController / tarmoq uzilishi
    assert b"event: delta" in first[1]
    row = _journal()[-1]
    assert (row["status"], row["answer_chars"]) == ("cancelled", 1)
    assert provider.closed == 1


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("published_synthetic", "enabled")
def test_journal_endpoint_for_auditors(make_client: Any) -> None:
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Servis — tayyorlash va SSE oqimi**

`backend/app/ai/service.py`:
```python
"""AI-анализ oqimi (TZ 7, S5 spec 4.1): tayyorlash (sinxron, threadpool) va SSE (asinxron).

Tayyorlash tartibi: funksiya yoqilganmi → savol → kontekst (foydalanuvchi RLS tranzaksiyasida)
→ tranzaksiya yopiladi (ulanish oqim davomida band qilinmaydi) → limit + jurnal yozuvi → audit.
Oqim: `start` → `delta`… → `done` | `error`; 60 s — umumiy muddat; mijoz uzilsa (generator
yopiladi) provayder oqimi yopiladi va jurnalga `cancelled` yoziladi. Prompt va javob matni log'ga
yozilmaydi.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import json
import time
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Any, Literal

from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.ai import journal
from app.ai.context import assemble_context, check_question
from app.ai.prompt import SYSTEM_PROMPT, build_prompt
from app.ai.provider import AI_TIMEOUT_S, AiProvider, AiProviderError
from app.ai.schemas import AiAnalyzeIn
from app.audit.service import AuditAction, log_event
from app.auth.context import AuthContext
from app.core.errors import AiDisabledError, AiLimitError
from app.core.request_meta import ClientMeta
from app.dwh.reference import current_setting

ERROR_TEXT = {
    "ai_timeout": "Время ожидания ответа истекло (60 секунд). Попробуйте сузить вопрос.",
    "ai_unavailable": "Сервис анализа временно недоступен. Повторите позже.",
    "ai_busy": "Сервис анализа перегружен. Повторите через минуту.",
    "ai_refused": "Модель отказалась отвечать на этот запрос. Переформулируйте вопрос.",
    "ai_misconfigured": "AI-анализ настроен неверно — обратитесь к администратору.",
    "ai_failed": "Не удалось получить ответ. Попробуйте ещё раз.",
}
_STATUS_BY_CODE = {"ai_timeout": "timeout", "ai_refused": "refused"}


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Job:
    request_id: int
    system: str
    prompt: str
    provider: AiProvider


def feature_state(conn: Connection,
                  provider: AiProvider | None) -> Literal["disabled", "not_configured"] | None:
    if current_setting(conn, "ai.enabled") is not True:
        return "disabled"
    if provider is None:
        return "not_configured"
    return None


def prepare(db: Session, ctx: AuthContext, body: AiAnalyzeIn, provider: AiProvider | None,
            meta: ClientMeta, *, today: date, now: datetime) -> Job:
    if provider is None or feature_state(db.connection(), provider) is not None:
        raise AiDisabledError()
    check_question(body.question)
    context = assemble_context(db, ctx, body, today=today, now=now)
    db.commit()  # RLS tranzaksiyasi tugaydi — ulanish oqim davomida ushlab turilmaydi
    filters: dict[str, Any] = {"br": body.br, "per": body.per}
    if body.section == "finmodel":
        filters["scen"] = body.scen
    base = {"section": body.section, "page": body.page, "filters": filters}
    try:
        request_id = journal.reserve(journal.NewRequest(
            ctx.user_id, body.section, body.page, filters, body.preset, body.question,
            context.chars, provider.name, provider.model), now=now)
    except AiLimitError as exc:
        log_event(meta, AuditAction.AI_REQUEST, user_id=ctx.user_id, result="denied",
                  object_type="ai_request",
                  details={**base, "reason": "limit", "scope": exc.params["scope"]})
        raise
    log_event(meta, AuditAction.AI_REQUEST, user_id=ctx.user_id, object_type="ai_request",
              object_id=request_id,
              details={**base, "preset": body.preset, "context_chars": context.chars,
                       "model": provider.model})
    prompt = build_prompt(context.text, preset=body.preset, question=body.question)
    return Job(request_id, SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt, provider)


def sse(event: str, data: dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:
    payload = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)
    return f"event: {event}\ndata: {payload}\n\n".encode()


async def stream_events(job: Job, *, timeout: float = AI_TIMEOUT_S,
                        clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic) -> AsyncIterator[bytes]:
    started = clock()
    deadline = started + timeout
    chars = 0
    status, code = "cancelled", None  # yopilish (mijoz uzildi) — standart natija
    chunks = job.provider.stream(job.system, job.prompt)
    try:
        yield sse("start", {"request_id": job.request_id})
        while True:
            remaining = deadline - clock()
            if remaining <= 0:
                raise TimeoutError
            try:
                part = await asyncio.wait_for(anext(chunks), remaining)
            except StopAsyncIteration:
                break
            chars += len(part)
            yield sse("delta", {"text": part})
        status = "ok"
        yield sse("done", {"length": chars})
    except TimeoutError:
        status, code = "timeout", "ai_timeout"
        yield sse("error", {"code": code, "message": ERROR_TEXT[code]})
    except AiProviderError as exc:
        status, code = _STATUS_BY_CODE.get(exc.code, "error"), exc.code
        yield sse("error", {"code": code, "message": ERROR_TEXT[code]})
    finally:
        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
            await chunks.aclose()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        duration = int((clock() - started) * 1000)
        with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
            await asyncio.shield(asyncio.to_thread(
                journal.finish, job.request_id, status=status, error_code=code,
                answer_chars=chars, duration_ms=duration))
```
(`chunks.aclose()` — async generator metodi; `AiProvider.stream` protokolda `AsyncIterator` deb e'lon qilingani uchun mypy `type: ignore[attr-defined]` talab qiladi. `asyncio.shield` — mijoz uzilishi paytidagi bekor qilish jurnal yozuvini to'xtatmasin.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Router**

`backend/app/ai/router.py`:
```python
"""AI-анализ HTTP API (TZ 7): holat, oqimli tahlil, jurnal (audit:read)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from starlette.concurrency import run_in_threadpool

from app.admin.schemas import Page
from app.ai import journal
from app.ai.provider import AiProvider, get_ai_provider
from app.ai.schemas import AiAnalyzeIn, AiRequestOut, AiStatusOut
from app.ai.service import feature_state, prepare, stream_events
from app.auth.context import AuthContext, Ctx, require_perm
from app.auth.permissions import Perm
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.core.request_meta import Meta
from app.sections.deps import Today

router = APIRouter(prefix="/ai", tags=["ai"])
journal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/audit", tags=["audit"])
DB = Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)]
Provider = Annotated[AiProvider | None, Depends(get_ai_provider)]
Auditor = Annotated[AuthContext, Depends(require_perm(Perm.AUDIT_READ))]


@router.get("/status", response_model=AiStatusOut)
def status(ctx: Ctx, db: DB, provider: Provider) -> AiStatusOut:
    conn = db.connection()
    reason = feature_state(conn, provider)
    per_user, per_org = journal.limits(conn)
    used = journal.used_last_hour(conn, ctx.user_id, datetime.now(UTC)) if reason is None else 0
    return AiStatusOut(enabled=reason is None, reason=reason, user_per_hour=per_user,
                       org_per_day=per_org, used_last_hour=used)


@router.post("/analyze", response_class=StreamingResponse,
             responses={200: {"content": {"text/event-stream": {}}}})
async def analyze(body: AiAnalyzeIn, ctx: Ctx, db: DB, provider: Provider, meta: Meta,
                  today: Today) -> StreamingResponse:
    job = await run_in_threadpool(prepare, db, ctx, body, provider, meta, today=today,
                                  now=datetime.now(UTC))
    return StreamingResponse(stream_events(job), media_type="text/event-stream",
                             headers={"Cache-Control": "no-store", "X-Accel-Buffering": "no"})


@journal_router.get("/ai-requests", response_model=Page[AiRequestOut])
def ai_requests(_: Auditor, db: DB, page: Annotated[int, Query(ge=1)] = 1,
                page_size: Annotated[int, Query(ge=1, le=500)] = 50) -> Page[AiRequestOut]:
    total = int(db.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM core.ai_requests")).scalar_one())
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT r.id, r.created_at, r.finished_at, u.username, r.section, r.page, r.filters, "
        "r.preset, r.question, r.status, r.error_code, r.context_chars, r.answer_chars, "
        "r.model, r.duration_ms FROM core.ai_requests r JOIN core.users u ON u.id = r.user_id "
        "ORDER BY r.id DESC LIMIT :l OFFSET :o"),
        {"l": page_size, "o": (page - 1) * page_size}).mappings().all()
    items = [AiRequestOut(**dict(r)) for r in rows]
    return Page[AiRequestOut](items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size)
```

```python
from app.ai.router import router as ai_router
```

### Task 11: Ekspluatatsiya — `compose.ai.yml` overlay, Caddy SSE, sirlar, egress, hujjatlar

**Files:**
- Create: `deploy/compose.ai.yml`, `docs/ops/ai-analiz.md`, `docs/security/ai-data-flow.md`, `backend/tests/ops/test_s5_ops.py`
- Modify: `deploy/compose.yml` (dev `api` — `AI_PROVIDER: fake`), `deploy/caddy/Caddyfile.dev`, `deploy/caddy/Caddyfile.prod`, `deploy/prod.env.example`, `deploy/secrets.example/README.md`, `scripts/prod_config.sh`, `docs/ops/host-hardening.md`
- Test: `backend/tests/ops/test_s5_ops.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 4-reja `compose.prod.yml` (`api` — `networks: [backend, web]`, `environment: *backend-env`, `secrets: [db_app_password, redis_password, master_kek, blind_index_key, session_pepper]`; `egress` tarmog'i — S2), `Caddyfile.prod`/`Caddyfile.dev` (`encode zstd gzip`, `handle /api/*`), `scripts/prod_config.sh`, `tests.ops.repo.repo_path`, 6–10-vazifalar sozlamalari (`AI_PROVIDER`, `AI_MODEL`, `AI_EFFORT`, `AI_FALLBACKS`, `AI_BASE_URL`, `EGRESS_PROXY`, sirlar `ai_api_key`, `ai_ca_bundle`).
- Produces: `deploy/compose.ai.yml` — faqat `api` servisini kengaytiradi (`egress` tarmog'i, `ai_api_key`/`ai_ca_bundle` sirlari, `AI_*` muhiti); standart `compose.prod.yml` o'zgarmaydi (api internetga chiqmaydi); Caddy: `/api/v1/ai/analyze` siqilmaydi va buferlanmaydi (`flush_interval -1`); dev'da soxta provayder; hujjatlar.

- [ ] **Step 1: Yiqiladigan testni yozish**

`backend/tests/ops/test_s5_ops.py`:
```python
from typing import Any

import pytest
import yaml

from tests.ops.repo import repo_path

AI_SECRETS = {"ai_api_key", "ai_ca_bundle"}
DOCS = {
    "docs/ops/ai-analiz.md": ("ai.enabled", "ai-enable", "compose.ai.yml", "ai_api_key",
                              "EGRESS_PROXY", "90", "claude-opus-5", "AI_MODEL",
                              "информационной безопасности", "/audit/ai-requests"),
    "docs/security/ai-data-flow.md": ("STRIDE", "14 000", "ai_context_rejected", "ФИО",
                                      "api.anthropic.com", "ai.enabled"),
}


def _load(path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    data: dict[str, Any] = yaml.safe_load(repo_path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return data


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def prod() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return _load("deploy/compose.prod.yml")


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def overlay() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return _load("deploy/compose.ai.yml")


def test_prod_api_is_offline_without_overlay(prod: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    api = prod["services"]["api"]
    assert "egress" not in api["networks"]
    for name, svc in prod["services"].items():
        assert not AI_SECRETS & set(svc.get("secrets", [])), name
        assert not any(k.startswith("AI_") for k in (svc.get("environment") or {})), name


def test_ai_overlay_extends_only_api(prod: dict[str, Any], overlay: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    assert set(overlay["services"]) == {"api"}
    api = overlay["services"]["api"]
    assert set(api["networks"]) == {"backend", "web", "egress"}
    assert set(api["secrets"]) == set(prod["services"]["api"]["secrets"]) | AI_SECRETS
    env = api["environment"]
    assert env["AI_PROVIDER"] == "anthropic"
    assert env["AI_MODEL"] == "${AI_MODEL:-claude-opus-5}"
    assert env["EGRESS_PROXY"] == "${EGRESS_PROXY:-}"
    assert not any("KEY" in key for key in env)
    for name in AI_SECRETS:
        assert name in overlay["secrets"]


def test_dev_uses_fake_provider() -> None:
    dev = _load("deploy/compose.yml")
    assert dev["services"]["api"]["environment"]["AI_PROVIDER"] == "fake"
    assert not AI_SECRETS & set(dev["services"]["api"].get("secrets", []))


def test_caddy_streams_ai_without_compression() -> None:
    for path in ("deploy/caddy/Caddyfile.dev", "deploy/caddy/Caddyfile.prod"):
        content = repo_path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "@compress not path /api/v1/ai/analyze" in content, path
        assert "encode @compress zstd gzip" in content, path
        assert "flush_interval -1" in content, path
        assert content.index("handle /api/v1/ai/analyze") < content.index("handle /api/*"), path


def test_docs_and_secrets() -> None:
    for path, needles in DOCS.items():
        content = repo_path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for needle in needles:
            assert needle in content, (path, needle)
    hardening = repo_path("docs/ops/host-hardening.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "api.anthropic.com" in hardening and "compose.ai.yml" in hardening
    readme = repo_path("deploy/secrets.example/README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "ai_api_key" in readme and "ai_ca_bundle" in readme
    script = repo_path("scripts/prod_config.sh").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "compose.ai.yml" in script and "ai_api_key" in script
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Compose — overlay va dev**

`deploy/compose.ai.yml`:
```yaml
# AI-анализ (S5, spec 4.3 va 6) — faqat axborot xavfsizligi xizmatining yozma roziligidan keyin:
#   docker compose -f deploy/compose.prod.yml -f deploy/compose.ai.yml --env-file deploy/prod.env up -d
# Standart compose.prod.yml da api internetga chiqmaydi. Bu overlay api ga egress tarmog'i
# (tavsiya — korporativ proksi EGRESS_PROXY orqali), ai_api_key siri va AI_* muhitini beradi.
# Funksiya shunda ham o'chiq qoladi, toki administrator `ai.enabled` ni yoqmaguncha
# (python -m app.cli ai-enable on --reason "…" yoki PUT /api/v1/admin/settings/ai.enabled).
services:
  api:
    networks: [backend, web, egress]
    environment:
      AI_PROVIDER: anthropic
      AI_MODEL: ${AI_MODEL:-claude-opus-5}
      AI_EFFORT: ${AI_EFFORT:-medium}
      AI_FALLBACKS: ${AI_FALLBACKS:-true}
      AI_BASE_URL: ${AI_BASE_URL:-}
      EGRESS_PROXY: ${EGRESS_PROXY:-}
    secrets: [db_app_password, redis_password, master_kek, blind_index_key, session_pepper,
              ai_api_key, ai_ca_bundle]

secrets:
  ai_api_key: {file: "${UGK_SECRETS_DIR:-./secrets}/ai_api_key"}
  ai_ca_bundle: {file: "${UGK_SECRETS_DIR:-./secrets}/ai_ca_bundle.pem"}
```
(Agar keyingi rejalar `compose.prod.yml` dagi `api.secrets` ro'yxatini kengaytirgan bo'lsa — overlay ro'yxati uning ustiga `ai_api_key, ai_ca_bundle` qo'shilgan to'liq nusxa bo'lishi kerak; test shuni tekshiradi.)

`deploy/compose.yml` — `api.environment` ga qo'shing:
```yaml
      # S5: dev'da AI — soxta provayder (tarmoqqa chiqmaydi). Baribir `ai.enabled` yoqilmaguncha o'chiq.
      AI_PROVIDER: fake
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Caddy — SSE siqilmaydi va buferlanmaydi**

`deploy/caddy/Caddyfile.prod` va `deploy/caddy/Caddyfile.dev` — har birida `encode zstd gzip` qatorini almashtiring:
```
	# S5: AI-анализ javobi — SSE oqimi; siqish uni buferlaydi, shuning uchun istisno.
	@compress not path /api/v1/ai/analyze
	encode @compress zstd gzip
```
va `handle /api/* {` blokidan OLDIN qo'shing:
```
	handle /api/v1/ai/analyze {
		reverse_proxy api:8000 {
			flush_interval -1
		}
	}
```
(Prod'da `/api/*` uchun `header /api/* ?Content-Disposition …` qatori o'zgarmaydi — u oqimga ta'sir qilmaydi.) Tekshirish: `make test-caddy` (4-reja, Caddyfile.prod xatti-harakati testi) — o'tadi.

- [ ] **Step 5: Muhit, sirlar, prod-config**

`deploy/prod.env.example` — oxiriga:
```bash
# AI-анализ (faqat deploy/compose.ai.yml bilan, ИБ roziligidan keyin). Model — sana qo'shimchasisiz ID.
AI_MODEL=claude-opus-5
AI_EFFORT=medium
AI_FALLBACKS=true
# Bo'sh — SDK standarti (https://api.anthropic.com). Faqat https.
AI_BASE_URL=
```
`deploy/secrets.example/README.md` — jadval oxiriga:
```markdown
| ai_api_key | Anthropic API kaliti (AI-анализ); faqat `api`, faqat `compose.ai.yml` bilan. Bo'sh — AI o'chiq |
| ai_ca_bundle.pem | ixtiyoriy korporativ CA (TLS-inspeksiyali proksi uchun); bo'sh fayl — tizim to'plami |
```
`scripts/prod_config.sh` — `for name in …` ro'yxatiga `ai_api_key ai_ca_bundle.pem` ni qo'shing (oxirgi element oldidan, ` \` davomi bilan) va `docker compose … config -q` qatoridan keyin qo'shing:
```bash
# S5: AI overlay'i bilan ham sintaksis va interpolatsiya.
UGK_VERSION=config-check UGK_SECRETS_DIR=../build/prod-config/secrets \
  docker compose -f deploy/compose.prod.yml -f deploy/compose.ai.yml \
  --env-file deploy/prod.env.example config -q
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Hujjatlar**

`docs/ops/ai-analiz.md`:
````markdown
# AI-анализ — включение, эксплуатация, журнал

**Статус по умолчанию: выключено.** Отправка даже агрегированных финансовых показателей внешнему
провайдеру (Anthropic, США) требует письменного согласования службы информационной безопасности
(требование национальной экспертизы). Без согласования этот раздел не выполняется.

## Как устроено
- Кнопка «AI-анализ» → сервер собирает сводку текущей страницы (только агрегаты, до 14 000 символов,
  без ФИО, ИНН, счетов, паспортов, наименований контрагентов и номеров документов), добавляет
  инструкцию и вызывает модель; ответ идёт потоком (SSE). Браузер модель напрямую не вызывает,
  ключ в браузер не передаётся.
- Модель по умолчанию — `claude-opus-5` (переменная `AI_MODEL`), официальный Python SDK `anthropic`,
  таймаут 60 секунд, повторные попытки SDK отключены.
- Ограничения: 20 запросов в час на пользователя (`ai.user_per_hour`), 300 в сутки на организацию
  (`ai.org_per_day`); при превышении — сообщение и время следующей попытки.
- Журнал `core.ai_requests`: дата, пользователь, раздел, страница, фильтры, вопрос, длина ответа,
  статус; хранение 90 дней (ежедневно в 04:20 задача `ai_journal` удаляет старые записи).
  Просмотр — `GET /api/v1/audit/ai-requests` и страница «Журнал AI-анализа» (право `audit:read`).
  В журнале аудита — событие `ai.request` (без текста вопроса).

## Включение (после согласования ИБ)
1. Получить ключ API в организации Anthropic, записать в `deploy/secrets/ai_api_key` (права 0600,
   `scripts/secrets_perms.sh`). При TLS-инспекции на прокси — сертификат ЦС в
   `deploy/secrets/ai_ca_bundle.pem` (иначе пустой файл).
2. Сетевой доступ: задать `EGRESS_PROXY` в `deploy/prod.env` и разрешить в прокси только
   `api.anthropic.com:443` (см. `docs/ops/host-hardening.md`).
3. Запуск с оверлеем: `docker compose -f deploy/compose.prod.yml -f deploy/compose.ai.yml
   --env-file deploy/prod.env up -d api`.
4. Включить функцию (аудитируемое изменение настройки):
   `docker compose … exec api python -m app.cli ai-enable on --reason "Письмо ИБ № … от …"`
   или администратор: `PUT /api/v1/admin/settings/ai.enabled {"value": true}`.
5. Проверка: в интерфейсе появляется кнопка «AI-анализ»; `GET /api/v1/ai/status` → `enabled: true`.

## Выключение и ротация ключа
- Выключить: `python -m app.cli ai-enable off --reason "…"` (кнопка исчезает сразу).
- Ротация: заменить файл `ai_api_key`, `docker compose … up -d api` (перезапуск api), старый ключ
  отозвать в консоли Anthropic.
- Полное отключение сети: запускать без `compose.ai.yml`.

## Смена модели
`AI_MODEL` (например, другая модель Claude) → перезапуск `api`. Идентификатор — без суффикса даты.
Изменение модели согласуется с ИБ так же, как включение.
````

`docs/security/ai-data-flow.md`:
````markdown
# AI-анализ: поток данных и угрозы (STRIDE)

## Поток
Браузер → (HTTPS, сессия + CSRF) → Caddy → api: `POST /api/v1/ai/analyze {раздел, страница, фильтры,
вопрос|шаблон}` → api собирает сводку из `pub.*` (RLS пользователя) → белый список полей → сканер
(ИНН/ПИНФЛ/счёт/паспорт, ключи ФИО/контрагент/реквизиты; при находке — отказ `ai_context_rejected`)
→ сводка ≤ 14 000 символов + инструкция → (TLS, прокси `EGRESS_PROXY`) → `api.anthropic.com` →
поток ответа → браузер. Ключ `ai_api_key` — Docker secret только у `api` и только с
`compose.ai.yml`; по умолчанию функция выключена (`ai.enabled = false`).

## Какие данные уходят
Только агрегаты текущей страницы (суммы, доли, названия филиалов, статей, станций, объектов,
кредиторов-банков), сработавшие правила (уровень, заголовок, значение), период, дата данных, вопрос
пользователя (без реквизитов — проверяется). Не уходят: первичные документы и их номера, ФИО,
паспортные данные, ИНН/ПИНФЛ, номера счетов, наименования контрагентов и поставщиков.

## STRIDE
| Угроза | Мера |
|---|---|
| S — подмена пользователя | сессия + TOTP, CSRF на POST, права раздела проверяются сервером |
| T — подмена контекста | контекст строится только на сервере из витрины; клиент передаёт лишь идентификаторы |
| R — отказ от действий | журнал `core.ai_requests` (90 дней) + событие аудита `ai.request` |
| I — утечка данных | белый список + сканер, RLS пользователя, выключено по умолчанию, egress только через прокси |
| D — отказ в обслуживании / расходы | 20/час, 300/сутки, таймаут 60 с, отмена, SDK-повторы выключены |
| E — повышение привилегий | вопрос пользователя и данные помечены как данные, модель не имеет инструментов |

## Остаточные риски
Ответ модели может быть неточным (в интерфейсе — оговорка о рекомендательном характере); условия
хранения данных у провайдера (ZDR) согласуются заказчиком; при необходимости — локальная модель
(предусмотрен интерфейс провайдера `AI_PROVIDER`).
````

`docs/ops/host-hardening.md` — S2 egress bo'limidan keyin qo'shing:
````markdown
**Выход в интернет для AI-анализа (раздел S5).** По умолчанию `api` в сеть `egress` не входит.
Только при запуске с `deploy/compose.ai.yml` (после согласования ИБ) `api` обращается к
`api.anthropic.com:443`. Рекомендуемый путь — тот же корпоративный прокси (`EGRESS_PROXY`) с
разрешённым доменом `api.anthropic.com`; правило `DOCKER-USER` для прокси уже задано для `worker`.
Без прокси — разрешить адреса `api.anthropic.com` на порт 443 (списки IP сопровождаются).
````

`frontend/src/router/hash.s5.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildHash, parseHash } from './hash';

describe('scenario in section routes', () => {
  it('parses a known scenario and keeps old routes unchanged', () => {
    expect(parseHash('#finmodel/dolg?br=2&scen=water')).toEqual({ kind: 'section', section: 'finmodel', page: 'dolg', br: 2, per: null, scen: 'water' });
    expect(parseHash('#finmodel/svod?scen=evil')).toEqual({ kind: 'section', section: 'finmodel', page: 'svod', br: null, per: null });
    expect(parseHash('#kazna/svod?per=q1')).toEqual({ kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'svod', br: null, per: 'q1' });
  });

  it('builds the scenario after branch and period', () => {
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'section', section: 'finmodel', page: 'svod', br: 3, per: 'm08', scen: 'fx' })).toBe('#finmodel/svod?br=3&per=m08&scen=fx');
    expect(buildHash({ kind: 'section', section: 'finmodel', page: 'svod', br: null, per: null, scen: null })).toBe('#finmodel/svod');
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/api/client.s5.test.ts`:
```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { json, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { sseController, sseResponse } from '../test/sse';
import { api, backgroundHeaders, setBackgroundMode } from './client';

afterEach(() => setBackgroundMode(false));

describe('background mode', () => {
  it('marks requests so the server does not extend the idle timeout', async () => {
    const { calls } = mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/svod': json({ ok: true }) });
    await api('/obzor/svod');
    expect(calls[0]?.headers.get('X-UGK-Background')).toBeNull();
    setBackgroundMode(true);
    expect(backgroundHeaders()).toEqual({ 'X-UGK-Background': '1' });
    await api('/obzor/svod');
    expect(calls[1]?.headers.get('X-UGK-Background')).toBe('1');
  });
});

describe('mock SSE helper', () => {
  it('streams raw event frames', async () => {
    const ctrl = sseController();
    mockApi({ 'POST /ai/analyze': sseResponse(ctrl) });
    ctrl.send('start', { request_id: 7 });
    ctrl.close();
    const response = await fetch('/api/v1/ai/analyze', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' });
    expect(response.headers.get('Content-Type')).toBe('text/event-stream');
    expect(await response.text()).toBe('event: start\ndata: {"request_id":7}\n\n');
  });
});
```

```ts
  const br = params.get('br');
  const per = params.get('per');
  const scen = params.get('scen');
  return {
    kind: 'section',
    section: first,
    page: second ?? null,
    br: br !== null && BR_RE.test(br) ? Number(br) : null,
    per: per !== null && PER_RE.test(per) ? per : null,
    ...(scen !== null && SCEN_RE.test(scen) ? { scen } : {}),
  };
```

```ts
/** S5: «Экран» (kiosk) rejimi so'rovlari sessiyaning idle muddatini uzaytirmaydi (server sarlavhani o'qiydi). */
export const BACKGROUND_HEADER = 'X-UGK-Background';
let backgroundMode = false;

export function setBackgroundMode(on: boolean): void {
  backgroundMode = on;
}

export function backgroundHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
  return backgroundMode ? { [BACKGROUND_HEADER]: '1' } : {};
}
```
- `function toApiError(` ni `export function toApiError(` ga almashtiring.
- `api()` ichida `const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: 'application/json' };` qatorini almashtiring:
```ts

```

`frontend/src/test/mockApi.ts`:
- `MockResponse` interfeysiga qo'shing: `/** JSON o'rniga xom tana (masalan, SSE oqimi). */ raw?: BodyInit;`
- `return new Response(…)` ni almashtiring:
```ts
    if (res.raw !== undefined) {
      return new Response(res.raw, { status, headers: { ...res.headers } });
    }
    return new Response(hasBody ? JSON.stringify(res.body) : null, {
      status,
      headers: { ...(hasBody ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}), ...res.headers },
    });
```

`frontend/src/test/sse.ts`:
```ts
import type { MockResponse } from './mockApi';

export interface SseController {
  stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>;
  send(event: string, data: unknown): void;
  close(): void;
  cancelled(): boolean;
}

/** Test boshqaradigan SSE oqimi: hodisalar `send` bilan, oxiri — `close`; klient bekor qilsa `cancelled()`. */
export function sseController(): SseController {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  let controller: ReadableStreamDefaultController<Uint8Array> | null = null;
  let wasCancelled = false;
  const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
    start(c) {
      controller = c;
    },
    cancel() {
      wasCancelled = true;
    },
  });
  return {
    stream,
    send(event, data) {
      controller?.enqueue(encoder.encode(`event: ${event}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`));
    },
    close() {
      controller?.close();
    },
    cancelled: () => wasCancelled,
  };
}

export function sseResponse(ctrl: SseController): MockResponse {
  return { status: 200, raw: ctrl.stream, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/event-stream' } };
}
```

```ts
  'formula.finCapacity': 'Мощность года = мощность предыдущего года + ввод по адресной программе',
  'formula.finRevenue': 'Выручка = отпуск в сеть × тариф года; отпуск = мощность × часы года × КИУМ сценария × (1 − собственные нужды)',
  'formula.finMargin': 'Рентабельность EBITDA = EBITDA / выручка; EBITDA = выручка − операционные расходы − налоги',
  'formula.finCapex': 'Инвестиции = план адресной программы по годам с поправкой сценария на курс',
  'formula.finCumFlow': 'Чистый поток = EBITDA − инвестиции − обслуживание долга; накопленный — сумма за 2026–2032 годы',
  'formula.finUnitCost': 'Себестоимость 1 кВт·ч = (операционные расходы + налоги) / отпуск в сеть',
  'formula.finNetFlow': 'Остаток на конец года = остаток предыдущего года + чистый поток; старт — фактический остаток на счетах',
```
(`formula.dscr` — S2'dan mavjud: «DSCR = EBITDA года / обслуживание долга года (из финансовой модели)».)

`frontend/src/test/s5Fixtures.ts`:
```ts
import type { AiRequestOut, AiStatusOut, FinBaseOut, FinmodelOut, FinSummaryOut, FinYearOut, Page } from '../api/types';

export const FIN_YEARS = [2026, 2027, 2028, 2029, 2030, 2031, 2032];
const DSCR = [1.35, 1.1, 1.5, 1.8, 2.0, 2.2, 2.4];

/** Yil qatori: summalar mln so'mda (5 trln → 8 trln tushum), 2027 — ковенант buzilishi, 2027 — manfiy qoldiq. */
export function finYear(year: number, over: Partial<FinYearOut> = {}): FinYearOut {
  const i = year - 2026;
  const revenue = 5_000_000 + i * 500_000;
  const opex = 2_000_000 + i * 100_000;
  const tax = revenue * 0.15;
  const ebitda = revenue - opex - tax;
  const capex = i === 1 ? 2_900_000 : 400_000;
  const dscr = DSCR[i] ?? 2;
  const debt = Math.round(ebitda / dscr);
  return {
    year,
    capacity_mw: [2000, 2120, 2330, 2425, 2485, 2525, 2555][i] ?? 2555,
    generation: 7000 + i * 300,
    supply: 6890 + i * 295,
    tariff: Math.round(705 * 1.08 ** i),
    revenue,
    opex,
    tax,
    ebitda,
    margin: ebitda / revenue,
    capex_plan: i >= 3 ? 0 : capex,
    capex,
    principal: Math.round(debt * 0.7),
    interest: debt - Math.round(debt * 0.7),
    debt_service: debt,
    net_flow: ebitda - capex - debt,
    balance: i === 1 ? -150_000 : 300_000 + i * 200_000,
    dscr,
    covenant_breach: dscr < 1.2,
    unit_cost: Math.round(((opex + tax) / (6890 + i * 295)) * 100) / 100,
    ...over,
  };
}

export const FIN_SUMMARY_BASE: FinSummaryOut = {
  scenario: 'base', title: 'Базовый', note: 'Приток воды в норме, тариф растёт по плану, курс стабилен',
  capacity_last: 2555, capacity_added: 555, revenue_first: 5_000_000, revenue_last: 8_000_000, ebitda_total: 16_000_000,
  margin_avg: 0.4, capex_total: 5_300_000, debt_service_total: 8_000_000, dscr_min: 1.1, dscr_min_year: 2027,
  cum_flow: 1_500_000, negative_years: 1, verdict: 'needs_financing',
};

export const FIN_SUMMARIES: FinSummaryOut[] = [
  FIN_SUMMARY_BASE,
  { ...FIN_SUMMARY_BASE, scenario: 'water', title: 'Маловодье', note: 'Выработка ниже нормы на 15% в 2027–2029 годах', cum_flow: 900_000, dscr_min: 0.95, revenue_last: 8_000_000 },
  { ...FIN_SUMMARY_BASE, scenario: 'tariff', title: 'Сдержанный тариф', note: 'Тариф растёт на 4% в год вместо 8%', cum_flow: 1_100_000, dscr_min: 1.05, revenue_last: 6_500_000 },
  { ...FIN_SUMMARY_BASE, scenario: 'fx', title: 'Ослабление сума', note: 'Курс растёт на 20%: дорожают валютные кредиты и импортное оборудование', cum_flow: 2_000_000, dscr_min: 1.3, negative_years: 0, verdict: 'funded' },
];

const BASE: FinBaseOut = {
  as_of: '2026-09-24', capacity_mw: 2000, opex_base: 2_000_000, opex_months: 12, opening_cash: 450_000,
  missing_fx: [], aip_missing_years: [2029, 2030, 2031, 2032], assumptions_at: '2026-09-20T10:00:00Z',
  stale_assumptions: false, covenant: 1.2,
};

export const FIN_MODEL: FinmodelOut = {
  has_model: true, scenario: 'base', company_wide: false, base: BASE,
  years: FIN_YEARS.map((y) => finYear(y)), summary: FIN_SUMMARY_BASE, scenarios: FIN_SUMMARIES,
  sensitivity: [
    { scenario: 'water', title: 'Маловодье', delta_cum: -600_000, dscr_min: 0.95 },
    { scenario: 'fx', title: 'Ослабление сума', delta_cum: 500_000, dscr_min: 1.3 },
    { scenario: 'tariff', title: 'Сдержанный тариф', delta_cum: -400_000, dscr_min: 1.05 },
  ],
};

export const FIN_MODEL_WATER: FinmodelOut = {
  ...FIN_MODEL, scenario: 'water', summary: FIN_SUMMARIES[1] ?? FIN_SUMMARY_BASE,
  years: FIN_YEARS.map((y) => finYear(y, y >= 2027 && y <= 2029 ? { generation: (7000 + (y - 2026) * 300) * 0.85 } : {})),
};

export const FIN_MODEL_STALE: FinmodelOut = {
  ...FIN_MODEL, company_wide: true,
  base: { ...BASE, stale_assumptions: true, missing_fx: ['XDR'], opex_months: 7 },
};

export const FIN_MODEL_EMPTY: FinmodelOut = {
  has_model: false, scenario: 'base', company_wide: false, base: null, years: [], summary: null, scenarios: [], sensitivity: [],
};

export const AI_STATUS_ON: AiStatusOut = { enabled: true, reason: null, user_per_hour: 20, org_per_day: 300, used_last_hour: 2 };
export const AI_STATUS_OFF: AiStatusOut = { enabled: false, reason: 'disabled', user_per_hour: 20, org_per_day: 300, used_last_hour: 0 };

const JOURNAL_ROW: AiRequestOut = {
  id: 41, created_at: '2026-09-24T09:15:00Z', finished_at: '2026-09-24T09:15:21Z', username: 'kazna.spec',
  section: 'kredity', page: 'svod', filters: { br: null, per: 'ytd' }, preset: null, question: 'Почему вырос долг?',
  status: 'ok', error_code: null, context_chars: 5210, answer_chars: 1830, model: 'claude-opus-5', duration_ms: 21000,
};

export const AI_JOURNAL: Page<AiRequestOut> = {
  items: [JOURNAL_ROW, { ...JOURNAL_ROW, id: 40, question: null, preset: 'risks', status: 'timeout', error_code: 'ai_timeout', answer_chars: 0 }],
  total: 2, page: 1, page_size: 50,
};
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Testlarni yozish**

`frontend/src/sections/finmodel/finmodel.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../../test/axe';
import { ME_ADMIN } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { json, mockApi } from '../../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../test/render';
import { FIN_MODEL, FIN_MODEL_EMPTY, FIN_MODEL_STALE, FIN_MODEL_WATER } from '../../test/s5Fixtures';
import FinDohody from './dohody';
import FinDolg from './dolg';
import FinScenarii from './scenarii';
import FinSvod from './svod';

const filters = { br: null, per: 'ytd' };
const props = (page: string) => ({ section: 'finmodel', page, filters, onBranch: vi.fn() });

function mockModel() {
  return mockApi({
    'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN),
    'GET /finmodel/model': (req) => json(req.url.searchParams.get('scen') === 'water' ? FIN_MODEL_WATER : FIN_MODEL),
  });
}

describe('Финмодель', () => {
  it('Сводка: six KPIs, charts, model table and base inputs', async () => {
    mockModel();
    const { container } = renderWithProviders(<FinSvod {...props('svod')} />, { hash: '#finmodel/svod' });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Мощность к 2032 году')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('ввод по программе +555 МВт')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('в 2027 году, порог 1,20')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('отрицательный остаток в 1 годах')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('img', { name: 'Выручка и EBITDA по годам' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('img', { name: 'Денежный поток по годам' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const table = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Модель по годам' });
    expect(within(table).getAllByRole('row')).toHaveLength(1 + 7 + 1);
    expect(within(table).getByText('Итого')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('План АИП не загружен на 2029, 2030, 2031, 2032 — инвестиции этих лет приняты равными нулю.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });

  it('switches the scenario through the URL and requests it', async () => {
    const { calls } = mockModel();
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<FinSvod {...props('svod')} />, { hash: '#finmodel/svod' });
    const group = await screen.findByRole('group', { name: 'Сценарий модели' });
    expect(within(group).getByRole('button', { name: 'Базовый' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    await user.click(within(group).getByRole('button', { name: 'Маловодье' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#finmodel/svod?scen=water'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(calls.some((c) => c.url.searchParams.get('scen') === 'water')).toBe(true));
    expect(within(group).getByRole('button', { name: 'Маловодье' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByText('Выработка ниже нормы на 15% в 2027–2029 годах')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('Доходы и расходы: revenue structure and cost per kWh', async () => {
    mockModel();
    renderWithProviders(<FinDohody {...props('dohody')} />, { hash: '#finmodel/dohody' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('img', { name: 'Структура выручки по годам' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const table = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Себестоимость и тариф' });
    expect(within(table).getAllByRole('row')).toHaveLength(1 + 7);
  });

  it('Инвестиции и долг: DSCR chart with the covenant and a highlighted breach', async () => {
    mockModel();
    renderWithProviders(<FinDolg {...props('dolg')} />, { hash: '#finmodel/dolg' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('img', { name: 'Покрытие долга (DSCR)' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const table = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Инвестиции и долговая нагрузка по годам' });
    const row2027 = within(table).getAllByRole('row')[2] as HTMLElement;
    expect(within(row2027).getByText('1,10')).toHaveClass('neg');
  });

  it('Сценарии: comparison table and sensitivity sorted by impact', async () => {
    mockModel();
    renderWithProviders(<FinScenarii {...props('scenarii')} />, { hash: '#finmodel/scenarii?scen=water' });
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Сравнение сценариев' });
    expect(within(table).getAllByRole('row')).toHaveLength(1 + 4);
    expect(within(table).getByText('Маловодье').closest('tr')).toHaveClass('is-active');
    expect(within(table).getAllByText('требует привлечения финансирования')).toHaveLength(3);
    const items = within(screen.getByRole('list', { name: 'Чувствительность' })).getAllByRole('listitem');
    expect(items[0]).toHaveTextContent('Маловодье');
  });

  it('shows stale assumptions and input warnings', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN), 'GET /finmodel/model': json(FIN_MODEL_STALE) });
    renderWithProviders(<FinSvod {...props('svod')} />, { hash: '#finmodel/svod?br=3' });
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Допущения изменены после расчёта/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Финансовая модель строится по компании в целом — фильтр филиала на неё не влияет.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Нет курса ЦБ для валют: XDR — эти кредиты не учтены в обслуживании долга.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('База расходов собрана за 7 мес. — история меньше года.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('explains the company-level model when nothing is visible', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN), 'GET /finmodel/model': json(FIN_MODEL_EMPTY) });
    renderWithProviders(<FinSvod {...props('svod')} />, { hash: '#finmodel/svod' });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Модель ещё не рассчитана')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Мощность к 2032 году')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { MessageKey } from '../../../i18n';
import { SCENARIO_IDS } from '../../../router/hash';
import { navigate, useRoute } from '../../../router/store';

export type Scenario = (typeof SCENARIO_IDS)[number];

export function isScenario(value: unknown): value is Scenario {
  return typeof value === 'string' && (SCENARIO_IDS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export const SCENARIO_TITLE_KEYS: Record<Scenario, MessageKey> = {
  base: 'fm.scen.base', water: 'fm.scen.water', tariff: 'fm.scen.tariff', fx: 'fm.scen.fx',
};

export const SCENARIO_NOTE_KEYS: Record<Scenario, MessageKey> = {
  base: 'fm.scen.note.base', water: 'fm.scen.note.water', tariff: 'fm.scen.note.tariff', fx: 'fm.scen.note.fx',
};

/** TZ 5: ssenariy har sahifada; URL'da `scen` (bazaviy — parametrsiz), boshqa filtrlar saqlanadi. */
export function useScenario(): { scen: Scenario; setScen(next: Scenario): void } {
  const route = useRoute();
  const scen: Scenario = route.kind === 'section' && isScenario(route.scen) ? route.scen : 'base';
  return {
    scen,
    setScen: (next) => {
      if (route.kind === 'section') navigate({ ...route, scen: next === 'base' ? null : next });
    },
  };
}
```

```tsx
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { SCENARIO_IDS } from '../../../router/hash';
import { SCENARIO_NOTE_KEYS, SCENARIO_TITLE_KEYS, type Scenario } from './scenario';

/** Prototip `scenBar`: to'rt ssenariy tugmasi va tanlangan ssenariy izohi. */
export function ScenarioBar({ current, onChange }: { current: Scenario; onChange(next: Scenario): void }) {
  return (
    <div className="toolbar">
      <span className="note">{t('fm.scen.label')}</span>
      <div className="seg" role="group" aria-label={t('fm.scen.group')}>
        {SCENARIO_IDS.map((id) => (
          <button key={id} type="button" aria-pressed={id === current} onClick={() => onChange(id)}>
            {t(SCENARIO_TITLE_KEYS[id])}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <span className="note">{t(SCENARIO_NOTE_KEYS[current])}</span>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/finmodel/parts/FinKpis.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { FinBaseOut, FinSummaryOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { formula } from '../../../dash/formulas';
import { formatMoney, formatPct, moneyParts } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { KpiGrid, KpiTile } from '../../../ui/KpiTile';

/** Prototip `kpisFin`: мощность 2032, выручка 2032, рентабельность, инвестиции, мин. DSCR, накопленный поток. */
export function FinKpis({ summary, base }: { summary: FinSummaryOut; base: FinBaseOut }) {
  const revenue = moneyParts(summary.revenue_last);
  const capex = moneyParts(summary.capex_total);
  const cum = moneyParts(summary.cum_flow);
  const lowDscr = summary.dscr_min !== null && summary.dscr_min < base.covenant;
  return (
    <KpiGrid>
      <KpiTile icon="bolt" label={t('fm.kpi.capacity')} value={formatNumber(summary.capacity_last, 0)} unit={t('fm.unit.mw')} delta={t('fm.kpi.capacityNote', { mw: formatNumber(summary.capacity_added, 0) })} hint={formula('finCapacity')} />
      <KpiTile icon="coins" label={t('fm.kpi.revenue')} value={revenue.value} unit={revenue.unit} delta={t('fm.kpi.revenueNote', { amount: formatMoney(summary.revenue_first) })} hint={formula('finRevenue')} />
      <KpiTile icon="net" label={t('fm.kpi.margin')} value={formatPct(summary.margin_avg, 1)} delta={t('fm.kpi.marginNote')} hint={formula('finMargin')} />
      <KpiTile icon="target" label={t('fm.kpi.capex')} value={capex.value} unit={capex.unit} delta={t('fm.kpi.capexNote')} hint={formula('finCapex')} />
      <KpiTile
        icon="pct"
        label={t('fm.kpi.dscr')}
        value={summary.dscr_min === null ? t('dash.noValue') : formatNumber(summary.dscr_min, 2)}
        negative={lowDscr}
        delta={summary.dscr_min_year === null ? t('fm.kpi.dscrNone') : t('fm.kpi.dscrNote', { year: summary.dscr_min_year, covenant: formatNumber(base.covenant, 2) })}
        hint={formula('dscr')}
      />
      <KpiTile
        icon="check"
        label={t('fm.kpi.cum')}
        value={cum.value}
        unit={cum.unit}
        negative={summary.cum_flow < 0}
        delta={summary.negative_years > 0 ? t('fm.kpi.cumNeg', { count: summary.negative_years }) : t('fm.kpi.cumOk')}
        hint={formula('finCumFlow')}
      />
    </KpiGrid>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/finmodel/parts/FinmodelFrame.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { FinBaseOut, FinmodelOut, FinSummaryOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import type { FilterState } from '../../../shell/useFilters';
import { EmptyState } from '../../../ui/EmptyState';
import { SectionData } from '../../SectionData';
import { useSection } from '../../useSection';
import { FinKpis } from './FinKpis';
import './finmodel.css';
import { ScenarioBar } from './ScenarioBar';
import { useScenario } from './scenario';

export type ReadyModel = FinmodelOut & { summary: FinSummaryOut; base: FinBaseOut };

/** Har sahifa: ssenariy almashtirgich (URL), so'rov, «по компании в целом», model yo'q holati, 6 KPI. */
export function FinmodelFrame({ filters, children }: { filters: FilterState; children(model: ReadyModel): ReactNode }) {
  const { scen, setScen } = useScenario();
  const query = useSection<FinmodelOut>('/finmodel/model', filters, { scen });
  return (
    <>
      <ScenarioBar current={scen} onChange={setScen} />
      <SectionData query={query}>
        {(data) =>
          !data.has_model || data.summary === null || data.base === null ? (
            <EmptyState icon="net" title={t('fm.noModel.title')} text={t('fm.noModel.text')} />
          ) : (
            <>
              {data.company_wide ? <p className="note fin-note">{t('fm.companyWide')}</p> : null}
              <FinKpis summary={data.summary} base={data.base} />
              {children({ ...data, summary: data.summary, base: data.base })}
            </>
          )
        }
      </SectionData>
    </>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/finmodel/parts/charts.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import type { FinYearOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import { barConfig, ChartCanvas, COLORS, type ChartHandle } from '../../../dash/chart';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { formula } from '../../../dash/formulas';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';

interface ChartProps {
  years: FinYearOut[];
  ctx: ExportContext;
  className?: string;
}

const labels = (years: FinYearOut[]) => years.map((y) => String(y.year));

function Legend({ items }: { items: Array<{ label: string; color: string; line?: boolean }> }) {
  return (
    <div className="legend">
      {items.map((x) => (
        <span key={x.label}>
          <i className={x.line ? 'is-line' : undefined} style={{ background: x.color }} />
          {x.label}
        </span>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Prototip `finRevChart`: выручка и EBITDA — ustunlar, рентабельность — chiziq o'ng o'qda. */
export function RevenueCard({ years, ctx, className }: ChartProps) {
  const title = t('fm.rev.title');
  const chartRef = useRef<ChartHandle | null>(null);
  const rev = COLORS.in();
  const ebitda = COLORS.v2();
  const margin = COLORS.bal();
  const config = useMemo(
    () =>
      barConfig({
        labels: labels(years),
        series: [
          { label: t('fm.rev.revenue'), data: years.map((y) => y.revenue), color: rev },
          { label: t('fm.rev.ebitda'), data: years.map((y) => y.ebitda), color: ebitda },
          { label: t('fm.rev.margin'), data: years.map((y) => (y.margin === null ? Number.NaN : y.margin * 100)), color: margin, type: 'line', axis: 'right' },
        ],
        rightFormat: (v, d) => `${formatNumber(v, d)}%`,
      }),
    [years, rev, ebitda, margin],
  );
  const table = { headers: [t('fm.col.year'), t('fm.rev.revenue'), t('fm.rev.ebitda'), t('fm.rev.margin')], rows: years.map((y) => [y.year, y.revenue, y.ebitda, y.margin === null ? null : Math.round(y.margin * 1000) / 10]) };
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title} sub={t('fm.rev.sub')} exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} chartRef={chartRef} csv={() => table} />}>
      <div title={formula('finMargin')}>
        <Legend items={[{ label: t('fm.rev.revenue'), color: rev }, { label: t('fm.rev.ebitda'), color: ebitda }, { label: t('fm.rev.margin'), color: margin, line: true }]} />
      </div>
      <ChartCanvas config={config} label={title} table={table} chartRef={chartRef} />
    </Card>
  );
}

/** Prototip `finCashChart`: EBITDA (+), инвестиции и обслуживание долга (−) — stacked; остаток — chiziq. */
export function CashFlowCard({ years, ctx, className }: ChartProps) {
  const title = t('fm.cash.title');
  const chartRef = useRef<ChartHandle | null>(null);
  const ebitda = COLORS.in();
  const capex = COLORS.prev();
  const debt = COLORS.v2();
  const balance = COLORS.bal();
  const config = useMemo(
    () =>
      barConfig({
        labels: labels(years),
        stacked: true,
        series: [
          { label: t('fm.rev.ebitda'), data: years.map((y) => y.ebitda), color: ebitda },
          { label: t('fm.cash.capex'), data: years.map((y) => -y.capex), color: capex },
          { label: t('fm.cash.debt'), data: years.map((y) => -y.debt_service), color: debt },
          { label: t('fm.cash.balance'), data: years.map((y) => y.balance), color: balance, type: 'line' },
        ],
      }),
    [years, ebitda, capex, debt, balance],
  );
  const table = { headers: [t('fm.col.year'), t('fm.rev.ebitda'), t('fm.cash.capex'), t('fm.cash.debt'), t('fm.col.net'), t('fm.cash.balance')], rows: years.map((y) => [y.year, y.ebitda, y.capex, y.debt_service, y.net_flow, y.balance]) };
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title} sub={t('fm.cash.sub')} exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} chartRef={chartRef} csv={() => table} />}>
      <div title={formula('finNetFlow')}>
        <Legend items={[{ label: t('fm.rev.ebitda'), color: ebitda }, { label: t('fm.cash.capex'), color: capex }, { label: t('fm.cash.debt'), color: debt }, { label: t('fm.cash.balance'), color: balance, line: true }]} />
      </div>
      <ChartCanvas config={config} label={title} table={table} chartRef={chartRef} />
    </Card>
  );
}

/** Prototip «Структура выручки»: операционные расходы, налоги и EBITDA (stacked). */
export function StructureCard({ years, ctx, className }: ChartProps) {
  const title = t('fm.struct.title');
  const chartRef = useRef<ChartHandle | null>(null);
  const opex = COLORS.v2();
  const tax = COLORS.prev();
  const ebitda = COLORS.in();
  const config = useMemo(
    () =>
      barConfig({
        labels: labels(years),
        stacked: true,
        series: [
          { label: t('fm.struct.opex'), data: years.map((y) => y.opex), color: opex },
          { label: t('fm.struct.tax'), data: years.map((y) => y.tax), color: tax },
          { label: t('fm.rev.ebitda'), data: years.map((y) => y.ebitda), color: ebitda },
        ],
      }),
    [years, opex, tax, ebitda],
  );
  const table = { headers: [t('fm.col.year'), t('fm.struct.opex'), t('fm.struct.tax'), t('fm.rev.ebitda'), t('fm.rev.revenue')], rows: years.map((y) => [y.year, y.opex, y.tax, y.ebitda, y.revenue]) };
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title} sub={t('fm.struct.sub')} exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} chartRef={chartRef} csv={() => table} />}>
      <Legend items={[{ label: t('fm.struct.opex'), color: opex }, { label: t('fm.struct.tax'), color: tax }, { label: t('fm.rev.ebitda'), color: ebitda }]} />
      <ChartCanvas config={config} label={title} table={table} chartRef={chartRef} />
    </Card>
  );
}

/** Prototip `fDscr`: DSCR chizig'i va ковенант chegarasi (uzuk-uzuk, qizil). */
export function DscrChartCard({ years, covenant, ctx, className }: ChartProps & { covenant: number }) {
  const title = t('fm.dscr.title');
  const chartRef = useRef<ChartHandle | null>(null);
  const line = COLORS.in();
  const threshold = t('fm.dscr.threshold', { covenant: formatNumber(covenant, 2) });
  const config = useMemo(
    () =>
      barConfig({
        labels: labels(years),
        format: (v, d) => formatNumber(v, Math.max(d, 1)),
        series: [
          { label: t('fm.col.dscr'), data: years.map((y) => y.dscr ?? Number.NaN), color: line, type: 'line' },
          { label: threshold, data: years.map(() => covenant), color: '#c9362c', type: 'line', dashed: true },
        ],
      }),
    [years, covenant, line, threshold],
  );
  const table = { headers: [t('fm.col.year'), t('fm.col.dscr')], rows: years.map((y) => [y.year, y.dscr]) };
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title} sub={t('fm.dscr.sub')} exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} chartRef={chartRef} csv={() => table} />}>
      <div title={formula('dscr')}>
        <Legend items={[{ label: t('fm.col.dscr'), color: line, line: true }, { label: threshold, color: '#c9362c', line: true }]} />
      </div>
      <ChartCanvas config={config} label={title} table={table} chartRef={chartRef} />
    </Card>
  );
}
```
(`#c9362c` — 3-reja `--neg` tokeni qiymati; Chart.js CSS o'zgaruvchisini o'qimaydi. S3'da `COLORS` ga `neg` qo'shilgan bo'lsa — `COLORS.neg()` ni ishlating.)

`frontend/src/sections/finmodel/parts/tables.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { FinYearOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { formula } from '../../../dash/formulas';
import { formatMoney, formatPct } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { Badge } from '../../../ui/Badge';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';
import { Table, type Column } from '../../../ui/Table';
import type { ReadyModel } from './FinmodelFrame';
import { isScenario, SCENARIO_NOTE_KEYS } from './scenario';

function Neg({ bad, children }: { bad: boolean; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <span className={bad ? 'neg' : undefined}>{children}</span>;
}

const dscrText = (v: number | null) => (v === null ? t('dash.noValue') : formatNumber(v, 2));
const sum = (years: FinYearOut[], pick: (y: FinYearOut) => number) => years.reduce((s, y) => s + pick(y), 0);

/** Prototip `finTable`: model yillar bo'yicha + «Итого». */
export function ModelTable({ model, ctx, className }: { model: ReadyModel; ctx: ExportContext; className?: string }) {
  const title = t('fm.table.title');
  const { years, summary, base } = model;
  const headers = [t('fm.col.year'), t('fm.col.capacity'), t('fm.col.generation'), t('fm.col.tariff'), t('fm.col.revenue'), t('fm.col.ebitda'), t('fm.col.margin'), t('fm.col.capex'), t('fm.col.debt'), t('fm.col.net'), t('fm.col.balance'), t('fm.col.dscr')];
  const columns: Column<FinYearOut>[] = [
    { key: 'year', header: headers[0] ?? '', cell: (y) => y.year, sortValue: (y) => y.year },
    { key: 'cap', header: headers[1] ?? '', cell: (y) => formatNumber(y.capacity_mw, 0), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'gen', header: headers[2] ?? '', cell: (y) => formatNumber(y.generation, 0), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'tariff', header: headers[3] ?? '', cell: (y) => formatNumber(y.tariff, 0), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'rev', header: headers[4] ?? '', cell: (y) => <b>{formatMoney(y.revenue)}</b>, align: 'right' },
    { key: 'ebitda', header: headers[5] ?? '', cell: (y) => formatMoney(y.ebitda), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'margin', header: headers[6] ?? '', cell: (y) => formatPct(y.margin, 1), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'capex', header: headers[7] ?? '', cell: (y) => formatMoney(y.capex), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'debt', header: headers[8] ?? '', cell: (y) => formatMoney(y.debt_service), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'net', header: headers[9] ?? '', cell: (y) => <Neg bad={y.net_flow < 0}>{formatMoney(y.net_flow)}</Neg>, align: 'right' },
    { key: 'bal', header: headers[10] ?? '', cell: (y) => <Neg bad={y.balance < 0}>{formatMoney(y.balance)}</Neg>, align: 'right' },
    { key: 'dscr', header: headers[11] ?? '', cell: (y) => <Neg bad={y.dscr !== null && y.dscr < base.covenant}>{dscrText(y.dscr)}</Neg>, align: 'right' },
  ];
  return (
    <Card
      className={className}
      title={title}
      sub={t('fm.table.sub')}
      exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} csv={() => ({ headers, rows: years.map((y) => [y.year, y.capacity_mw, y.generation, y.tariff, y.revenue, y.ebitda, y.margin, y.capex, y.debt_service, y.net_flow, y.balance, y.dscr]) })} />}
    >
      <div title={formula('finRevenue')}>
        <Table
          caption={title}
          columns={columns}
          rows={years}
          rowKey={(y) => y.year}
          free
          footer={
            <tr>
              <td>{t('fm.total')}</td>
              <td colSpan={3} />
              <td>{formatMoney(sum(years, (y) => y.revenue))}</td>
              <td>{formatMoney(summary.ebitda_total)}</td>
              <td>{formatPct(summary.margin_avg, 1)}</td>
              <td>{formatMoney(summary.capex_total)}</td>
              <td>{formatMoney(summary.debt_service_total)}</td>
              <td>{formatMoney(summary.cum_flow)}</td>
              <td />
              <td>{dscrText(summary.dscr_min)}</td>
            </tr>
          }
        />
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

/** Prototip «Себестоимость и тариф»: на 1 кВт·ч отпущенной энергии. */
export function CostTariffTable({ years, className }: { years: FinYearOut[]; className?: string }) {
  const title = t('fm.cost.title');
  const columns: Column<FinYearOut>[] = [
    { key: 'year', header: t('fm.col.year'), cell: (y) => y.year },
    { key: 'tariff', header: t('fm.col.tariff'), cell: (y) => formatNumber(y.tariff, 0), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'cost', header: t('fm.col.cost'), cell: (y) => (y.unit_cost === null ? t('dash.noValue') : formatNumber(y.unit_cost, 0)), align: 'right' },
    {
      key: 'margin',
      header: t('fm.col.kwhMargin'),
      cell: (y) => (y.unit_cost === null ? t('dash.noValue') : <Neg bad={y.tariff - y.unit_cost < 0}>{formatNumber(y.tariff - y.unit_cost, 0)}</Neg>),
      align: 'right',
    },
  ];
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title} sub={t('fm.cost.sub')}>
      <div title={formula('finUnitCost')}>
        <Table caption={title} columns={columns} rows={years} rowKey={(y) => y.year} free />
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

/** Prototip «Инвестиции и долговая нагрузка по годам». */
export function DebtTable({ years, covenant, className }: { years: FinYearOut[]; covenant: number; className?: string }) {
  const title = t('fm.debt.title');
  const columns: Column<FinYearOut>[] = [
    { key: 'year', header: t('fm.col.year'), cell: (y) => y.year },
    { key: 'capex', header: t('fm.col.capex'), cell: (y) => formatMoney(y.capex), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'principal', header: t('fm.col.principal'), cell: (y) => formatMoney(y.principal), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'interest', header: t('fm.col.interest'), cell: (y) => formatMoney(y.interest), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'debt', header: t('fm.col.debt'), cell: (y) => formatMoney(y.debt_service), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'ebitda', header: t('fm.col.ebitda'), cell: (y) => formatMoney(y.ebitda), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'dscr', header: t('fm.col.dscr'), cell: (y) => <Neg bad={y.dscr !== null && y.dscr < covenant}>{dscrText(y.dscr)}</Neg>, align: 'right' },
    { key: 'free', header: t('fm.col.free'), cell: (y) => <Neg bad={y.net_flow < 0}>{formatMoney(y.net_flow)}</Neg>, align: 'right' },
  ];
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title}>
      <Table caption={title} columns={columns} rows={years} rowKey={(y) => y.year} free />
    </Card>
  );
}

/** Prototip `scenTable`: to'rt ssenariy, tanlangani ajratilgan, xulosa belgisi. */
export function ScenarioTable({ model, className }: { model: ReadyModel; className?: string }) {
  const title = t('fm.scenTable.title');
  const covenant = model.base.covenant;
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title} sub={t('fm.scenTable.sub')}>
      <div className="tw tw--free">
        <table className="table">
          <caption className="visually-hidden">{title}</caption>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th scope="col" className="l">{t('fm.scenTable.scenario')}</th>
              <th scope="col">{t('fm.scenTable.revenue')}</th>
              <th scope="col">{t('fm.scenTable.ebitda')}</th>
              <th scope="col">{t('fm.col.margin')}</th>
              <th scope="col">{t('fm.kpi.dscr')}</th>
              <th scope="col">{t('fm.scenTable.cum')}</th>
              <th scope="col" className="l">{t('fm.scenTable.verdict')}</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {model.scenarios.map((s) => (
              <tr key={s.scenario} className={s.scenario === model.scenario ? 'is-active' : undefined}>
                <td className="l">
                  {s.title}
                  <br />
                  <span className="note">{isScenario(s.scenario) ? t(SCENARIO_NOTE_KEYS[s.scenario]) : s.note}</span>
                </td>
                <td>{formatMoney(s.revenue_last)}</td>
                <td>{formatMoney(s.ebitda_total)}</td>
                <td>{formatPct(s.margin_avg, 1)}</td>
                <td><Neg bad={s.dscr_min !== null && s.dscr_min < covenant}>{dscrText(s.dscr_min)}</Neg></td>
                <td><Neg bad={s.cum_flow < 0}>{formatMoney(s.cum_flow)}</Neg></td>
                <td className="l">
                  <Badge tone={s.verdict === 'funded' ? 'blue' : 'neg'}>{t(s.verdict === 'funded' ? 'fm.verdict.funded' : 'fm.verdict.needs_financing')}</Badge>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}
```
(Ssenariylar jadvali — oddiy `<table>` (qator bosish/saralash kerak emas), 3-reja `table`/`tw` klasslari; `Table` komponenti qator uchun klass qabul qilmaydi.)

`frontend/src/sections/finmodel/parts/BaseInfoCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { FinBaseOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { formatMoney } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatDate, formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';

/** Model kirishlari (spec 3.3) va ogohlantirishlar: eskirgan допущения, qisqa tarix, kurs yo'q, АИП yo'q. */
export function BaseInfoCard({ base, className }: { base: FinBaseOut; className?: string }) {
  const warnings = [
    base.stale_assumptions ? t('fm.base.stale') : null,
    base.opex_months < 12 ? t('fm.base.opexShort', { months: base.opex_months }) : null,
    base.missing_fx.length ? t('fm.base.missingFx', { list: base.missing_fx.join(', ') }) : null,
    base.aip_missing_years.length ? t('fm.base.aipMissing', { years: base.aip_missing_years.join(', ') }) : null,
  ].filter((x): x is string => x !== null);
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={t('fm.base.title')} sub={t('fm.base.sub', { date: formatDate(base.as_of) })}>
      <ul className="fin-base">
        <li><span>{t('fm.base.capacity')}</span><b>{formatNumber(base.capacity_mw, 0)} {t('fm.unit.mw')}</b></li>
        <li><span>{t('fm.base.opex')}</span><b>{formatMoney(base.opex_base)}</b></li>
        <li><span>{t('fm.base.cash')}</span><b>{formatMoney(base.opening_cash)}</b></li>
        <li><span>{t('fm.base.covenant')}</span><b>{formatNumber(base.covenant, 2)}</b></li>
      </ul>
      {warnings.length ? (
        <ul className="fin-warn" role="status">
          {warnings.map((w) => <li key={w}>{w}</li>)}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/finmodel/parts/SensitivityCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { formatMoney } from '../../../dash/money';
import { Rank } from '../../../dash/Rank';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';
import type { ReadyModel } from './FinmodelFrame';

/** Prototip «Чувствительность»: jamg'arilgan oqimning bazaviy ssenariyga nisbatan o'zgarishi. */
export function SensitivityCard({ model, className }: { model: ReadyModel; className?: string }) {
  const title = t('fm.sens.title');
  const items = model.sensitivity.map((x) => ({
    id: x.scenario,
    name: x.title,
    value: Math.abs(x.delta_cum),
    negative: x.delta_cum < 0,
    note: t('fm.sens.note', { sign: x.delta_cum < 0 ? '−' : '+', amount: formatMoney(Math.abs(x.delta_cum)), dscr: x.dscr_min === null ? t('dash.noValue') : formatNumber(x.dscr_min, 2) }),
  }));
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title} sub={t('fm.sens.sub')}>
      <Rank label={title} items={items} />
    </Card>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { FinAssumptionOut } from '../api/types';
import { ME_ADMIN } from '../test/fixtures';
import { json, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../test/render';
import FinAssumptionsPage from './FinAssumptionsPage';

const at = '2026-09-01T00:00:00Z';
const row = (year: number, key: string, value: number): FinAssumptionOut => ({ scenario: 'base', year, key, value, version: 1, updated_by: null, updated_at: at });
const FIN: FinAssumptionOut[] = [
  row(2026, 'tariff_growth', 0.08), row(2027, 'tariff_growth', 0.08),
  row(2026, 'capacity_add_mw', 0), row(2027, 'capacity_add_mw', 120),
  row(2026, 'tariff_start', 705),
];

describe('FinAssumptionsPage (S5)', () => {
  it('shows new keys, scalar keys only in 2026 and the second-year note', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /admin/fin-assumptions': json(FIN) });
    renderWithProviders(<FinAssumptionsPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Ввод мощностей по АИП, МВт, 2027')).toHaveValue('120');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Тариф 2026 года, сум/кВт·ч, 2026')).toHaveValue('705');
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Тариф 2026 года, сум/кВт·ч, 2027')).toBeNull();
    const tariffRow = screen.getByRole('row', { name: /Тариф 2026 года/ });
    expect(within(tariffRow).getAllByText('—')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(screen.getByText(/применяются со второго года модели \(2027\)/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('validates each key against its own range', async () => {
    const server = mockApi({ 'GET /admin/fin-assumptions': json(FIN), 'PUT /admin/fin-assumptions': json(FIN) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<FinAssumptionsPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    const tariff = await screen.findByLabelText('Тариф 2026 года, сум/кВт·ч, 2026');
    await user.clear(tariff);
    await user.type(tariff, '0');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сохранить изменения' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Значение вне допустимого диапазона: Базовый · Тариф 2026 года, сум/кВт·ч · 2026');
    await user.clear(tariff);
    await user.type(tariff, '750');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Сохранить изменения' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Допущения сохранены. Изменение записано в журнал аудита.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'PUT')?.body).toEqual({ items: [{ scenario: 'base', year: 2026, key: 'tariff_start', value: 750 }] });
  });

  it('requests a rebuild only for users with admin:etl', async () => {
    const server = mockApi({ 'GET /admin/fin-assumptions': json(FIN), 'POST /admin/etl/run': json({ request_id: 5, status: 'pending' }, 202) });
    const { user, unmount } = renderWithProviders(<FinAssumptionsPage me={ME_ADMIN} />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Пересчитать модель' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Пересчёт поставлен в очередь загрузок/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(server.calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ source: 'rebuild', mode: 'full' });
    unmount();
    const noEtl = { ...ME_ADMIN, permissions: ME_ADMIN.permissions.filter((p) => p !== 'admin:etl') };
    renderWithProviders(<FinAssumptionsPage me={noEtl} />);
    await screen.findByLabelText('Рост тарифа, 2027');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Пересчитать модель' })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```
(Qator nomi `getByRole('row', { name: … })` — birinchi ustun `<th scope="row">` kalit nomi, 3-reja jadvali.)

- `KEY_TITLES` dan keyin qo'shing:
```tsx
/** S5: backend `FIN_KEY_BOUNDS` bilan bir xil oraliqlar (tekshiruv baribir serverda). */
const KEY_BOUNDS: Record<string, readonly [number, number]> = {
  tariff_growth: [-0.5, 1],
  generation_factor: [0, 2],
  fx_rate_factor: [0.1, 5],
  opex_index: [-0.5, 1],
  tax_rate: [0, 0.9],
  kium: [0.01, 1],
  capacity_add_mw: [0, 5000],
  tariff_start: [1, 100000],
  own_needs_share: [0, 0.5],
  capex_fx_share: [0, 1],
};

function inBounds(key: string, value: number): boolean {
  const [low, high] = KEY_BOUNDS[key] ?? [-10, 10];
  return value >= low && value <= high;
}
```

```tsx

```
va `const toast = useToast();` dan keyin qo'shing:

```tsx
  const canRebuild = me?.permissions.includes('admin:etl') ?? false;
```

  - `AiDrawer({ menu, route, onClose })` — holatlar: `idle`, `streaming` (qisman matn, «Отменить»), `done` (izoh), `error`, `limit` (keyingi urinish vaqti, user/org), `cancelled`, `disabled`; 4 tayyor savol, o'z savoli (≤ 500 belgi), 65 s klient chegarasi.
  - `AiButton({ menu, route })` — faqat bo'lim sahifasida va `status.enabled` bo'lsa.
  - `AdminPageId` += `'aijournal'` (`audit:read`), `AiJournalPage`.

`frontend/src/ai/stream.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';
import { apiError, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { sseController, sseResponse } from '../test/sse';
import { parseFrame, streamAnalysis, type AiEvent } from './stream';

const body = { section: 'obzor', page: 'svod', br: null, per: 'ytd' };

describe('AI SSE client', () => {
  it('parses frames', () => {
    expect(parseFrame('event: start\ndata: {"request_id":5}')).toEqual({ type: 'start', requestId: 5 });
    expect(parseFrame('event: delta\ndata: {"text":"## Итог\\n"}')).toEqual({ type: 'delta', text: '## Итог\n' });
    expect(parseFrame('event: error\ndata: {"code":"ai_timeout","message":"m"}')).toEqual({ type: 'error', code: 'ai_timeout', message: 'm' });
    expect(parseFrame(': keepalive')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('delivers events split across chunks and sends CSRF', async () => {
    const ctrl = sseController();
    const { calls } = mockApi({ 'POST /ai/analyze': sseResponse(ctrl) });
    document.cookie = '__Host-ugk_csrf=tok; Secure; Path=/';
    const got: AiEvent[] = [];
    const done = streamAnalysis(body, (e) => got.push(e), new AbortController().signal);
    ctrl.send('start', { request_id: 1 });
    ctrl.send('delta', { text: 'Привет' });
    ctrl.send('done', { length: 6 });
    ctrl.close();
    await done;
    expect(got).toEqual([{ type: 'start', requestId: 1 }, { type: 'delta', text: 'Привет' }, { type: 'done', length: 6 }]);
    expect(calls[0]?.headers.get('X-CSRF-Token')).toBe('tok');
    expect(calls[0]?.body).toEqual(body);
  });

  it('throws ApiError before the stream (limit) and cancels the reader on abort', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'POST /ai/analyze': apiError(429, 'ai_limit', 'Лимит', { retry_after: 120, scope: 'org' }, { 'Retry-After': '120' }) });
    await expect(streamAnalysis(body, () => undefined, new AbortController().signal)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429, code: 'ai_limit', retryAfter: 120 });
    const ctrl = sseController();
    mockApi({ 'POST /ai/analyze': sseResponse(ctrl) });
    const abort = new AbortController();
    const running = streamAnalysis(body, () => undefined, abort.signal);
    ctrl.send('start', { request_id: 2 });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    abort.abort();
    await running.catch((error: unknown) => expect(error).not.toBeInstanceOf(ApiError));
    expect(ctrl.cancelled()).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import type userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';

import type { Route } from '../router/hash';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../test/axe';

const route: Route = { kind: 'section', section: 'kredity', page: 'svod', br: null, per: null };

async function openDrawer(user: ReturnType<typeof userEvent.setup>) {
  await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'AI-анализ' }));
  return screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'AI-анализ' });
}

describe('AI-анализ', () => {

    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'AI-анализ' })).toBeNull();
  });
```
