```tsx
    const ctrl = sseController();
    const { calls } = mockApi({ ...shellRoutes(), 'GET /ai/status': json(AI_STATUS_ON), 'POST /ai/analyze': sseResponse(ctrl) });
    const { user, container } = renderWithProviders(<AiButton menu={MENU_FULL} route={route} />);
    const dialog = await openDrawer(user);
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('Кредиты: Сводка', { exact: false })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Проанализировать' }));
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('Claude анализирует данные страницы…')).toBeInTheDocument();
    ctrl.send('start', { request_id: 9 });
    ctrl.send('delta', { text: '## Что важно заметить\n- Портфель **4,2 трлн сум**\n' });
    expect(await within(dialog).findByRole('heading', { name: 'Что важно заметить' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('4,2 трлн сум').tagName).toBe('B');
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Отменить' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    ctrl.send('done', { length: 40 });
    ctrl.close();
    await waitFor(() => expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('button', { name: 'Отменить' })).toBeNull());
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('Ответ носит рекомендательный характер и не заменяет решение ответственного лица.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ section: 'kredity', page: 'svod', br: null, per: 'ytd' });
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container.ownerDocument.body);
  });

  it('shows provider errors and drops the partial text', async () => {
    const ctrl = sseController();
    mockApi({ ...shellRoutes(), 'GET /ai/status': json(AI_STATUS_ON), 'POST /ai/analyze': sseResponse(ctrl) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<AiButton menu={MENU_FULL} route={route} />);
    const dialog = await openDrawer(user);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Проанализировать' }));
    ctrl.send('delta', { text: 'Частичный ответ' });
    ctrl.send('error', { code: 'ai_refused', message: 'Модель отказалась отвечать на этот запрос.' });
    ctrl.close();
    expect(await within(dialog).findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Модель отказалась отвечать на этот запрос.');
    expect(within(dialog).queryByText('Частичный ответ')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('explains the limit with the next attempt time and the organisation scope', async () => {
    mockApi({
      ...shellRoutes(),
      'GET /ai/status': json(AI_STATUS_ON),
      'POST /ai/analyze': apiError(429, 'ai_limit', 'Лимит', { retry_after: 600, scope: 'org' }, { 'Retry-After': '600' }),
    });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<AiButton menu={MENU_FULL} route={route} />);

    expect(await within(dialog).findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(/Лимит запросов организации исчерпан \(300 в сутки\)\. Следующая попытка — в \d{2}:\d{2}\./);
  });

  it('cancel aborts the stream and keeps the drawer usable', async () => {
    const ctrl = sseController();
    const { calls } = mockApi({ ...shellRoutes(), 'GET /ai/status': json(AI_STATUS_ON), 'POST /ai/analyze': sseResponse(ctrl) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<AiButton menu={MENU_FULL} route={route} />);
    const dialog = await openDrawer(user);
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Свой вопрос по данным страницы' }), 'Почему вырос долг?');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Спросить' }));
    ctrl.send('start', { request_id: 3 });
    await user.click(await within(dialog).findByRole('button', { name: 'Отменить' }));
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('Запрос отменён.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(ctrl.cancelled()).toBe(true);
    expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toMatchObject({ question: 'Почему вырос долг?' });
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Проанализировать' })).toBeEnabled();
  });

  it('reports a feature switched off during the session', async () => {
    mockApi({ ...shellRoutes(), 'GET /ai/status': json(AI_STATUS_ON), 'POST /ai/analyze': apiError(404, 'ai_disabled', 'AI-анализ отключён') });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<AiButton menu={MENU_FULL} route={route} />);
    const dialog = await openDrawer(user);
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Проанализировать' }));
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('AI-анализ отключён администратором.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { useQuery, type UseQueryResult } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import type { AiStatusOut } from '../api/types';

/** Funksiya yoqilganmi (sozlama + provayder). Xato jimgina — tugma shunchaki ko'rinmaydi. */
export function useAiStatus(enabled: boolean): UseQueryResult<AiStatusOut> {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['ai', 'status'],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<AiStatusOut>('/ai/status', { signal }),
    enabled,
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
    meta: { silent: true },
  });
}
```

`frontend/src/ai/stream.ts`:
```ts
import { ApiError, apiUrl, backgroundHeaders, CSRF_COOKIE, CSRF_HEADER, readCookie, toApiError } from '../api/client';
import { t } from '../i18n';

export type AiEvent =
  | { type: 'start'; requestId: number }
  | { type: 'delta'; text: string }
  | { type: 'done'; length: number }
  | { type: 'error'; code: string; message: string };

export interface AiRequestBody {
  section: string;
  page: string;
  br: number | null;
  per: string;
  scen?: string;
  preset?: string;
  question?: string;
}

/** Bitta SSE freymi (`event:` + `data:` qatorlari). Izoh yoki noma'lum hodisa — `null`. */
export function parseFrame(frame: string): AiEvent | null {
  let name = '';
  let data = '';
  for (const line of frame.split('\n')) {
    if (line.startsWith('event: ')) name = line.slice(7).trim();
    else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) data += line.slice(6);
  }
  if (!name || !data) return null;
  const payload = JSON.parse(data) as Record<string, unknown>;
  switch (name) {
    case 'start':
      return { type: 'start', requestId: Number(payload.request_id) };
    case 'delta':
      return { type: 'delta', text: String(payload.text ?? '') };
    case 'done':
      return { type: 'done', length: Number(payload.length ?? 0) };
    case 'error':
      return { type: 'error', code: String(payload.code ?? 'ai_failed'), message: String(payload.message ?? '') };
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

/** TZ 7: javob oqim bilan (POST + SSE — EventSource POST/CSRF qo'llamaydi). Bekor qilish — `signal`. */
export async function streamAnalysis(body: AiRequestBody, onEvent: (event: AiEvent) => void, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { Accept: 'text/event-stream', 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...backgroundHeaders() };
  const token = readCookie(CSRF_COOKIE);
  if (token) headers[CSRF_HEADER] = token;
  let response: Response;
  try {
    response = await fetch(apiUrl('/ai/analyze'), { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body), credentials: 'same-origin', signal });
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error as { name?: string } | null)?.name === 'AbortError') throw error;
    throw new ApiError(0, 'network', t('error.network'));
  }
  if (!response.ok || response.body === null) {
    const payload: unknown = await response.json().catch(() => null);
    throw toApiError(response, payload);
  }
  const reader = response.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  const onAbort = () => {
    void reader.cancel();
  };
  signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort);
  let buffer = '';
  try {
    for (;;) {
      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
      let end = buffer.indexOf('\n\n');
      while (end >= 0) {
        const event = parseFrame(buffer.slice(0, end));
        buffer = buffer.slice(end + 2);
        if (event) onEvent(event);
        end = buffer.indexOf('\n\n');
      }
    }
  } finally {
    signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/ai/answer.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

function inline(text: string): ReactNode[] {
  return text
    .split(/(\*\*[^*]+\*\*)/g)
    .filter((part) => part !== '')
    .map((part, i) => (part.startsWith('**') && part.endsWith('**') && part.length > 4 ? <b key={i}>{part.slice(2, -2)}</b> : part));
}

const BULLET = /^\s*(?:[-*•]|\d+[.)])\s+/;

/** Model javobi: `## ` — sarlavha, `- `/`1. ` — ro'yxat, `**…**` — qalin. HTML hech qachon kiritilmaydi. */
export function AiAnswer({ text }: { text: string }) {
  const blocks: ReactNode[] = [];
  let items: ReactNode[] = [];
  const flush = () => {
    if (items.length) blocks.push(<ul key={`list-${blocks.length}`}>{items}</ul>);
    items = [];
  };
  text.split('\n').forEach((raw, i) => {
    const line = raw.trimEnd();
    if (line.startsWith('## ')) {
      flush();
      blocks.push(<h3 key={i}>{inline(line.slice(3))}</h3>);
    } else if (BULLET.test(line)) {
      items.push(<li key={i}>{inline(line.replace(BULLET, ''))}</li>);
    } else if (line.trim() === '') {
      flush();
    } else {
      flush();
      blocks.push(<p key={i}>{inline(line)}</p>);
    }
  });
  flush();
  return <div className="ai-answer">{blocks}</div>;
}
```

`frontend/src/ai/AiDrawer.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { ApiError, isApiError } from '../api/client';
import type { MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime } from '../i18n/format';
import { useBranches, usePeriods } from '../meta/api';
import type { SectionRoute } from '../router/hash';
import { isScenario } from '../sections/finmodel/parts/scenario';
import { flattenMenu } from '../shell/sectionMeta';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Drawer } from '../ui/Drawer';
import { AiAnswer } from './answer';
import './ai.css';
import { streamAnalysis, type AiRequestBody } from './stream';

const CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS = 65_000; // server 60 s + tarmoq zaxirasi
const PRESETS: ReadonlyArray<{ id: string; key: MessageKey }> = [
  { id: 'yoy', key: 'ai.preset.yoy' },
  { id: 'risks', key: 'ai.preset.risks' },
  { id: 'priorities', key: 'ai.preset.priorities' },
  { id: 'plan', key: 'ai.preset.plan' },
];

type Phase =
  | { kind: 'idle' }
  | { kind: 'streaming' }
  | { kind: 'done' }
  | { kind: 'error'; message: string }
  | { kind: 'limit'; message: string }
  | { kind: 'cancelled' }
  | { kind: 'disabled' };

function nextAttempt(seconds: number): string {
  return formatDateTime(new Date(Date.now() + seconds * 1000)).slice(-5);
}

export interface AiDrawerProps {
  menu: MenuOut;
  route: SectionRoute;
  onClose(): void;
}

export function AiDrawer({ menu, route, onClose }: AiDrawerProps) {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>({ kind: 'idle' });
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState('');
  const controllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const timedOut = useRef(false);
  const periods = usePeriods();
  const branches = useBranches();

  useEffect(() => () => controllerRef.current?.abort(), []);

  const section = flattenMenu(menu).find((s) => s.id === route.section);
  const pageTitle = section?.pages.find((p) => p.id === route.page)?.title ?? '';
  const per = route.per ?? periods.data?.default ?? 'ytd';
  const branchName = route.br === null ? t('filters.allBranches') : (branches.data?.find((b) => b.id === route.br)?.name ?? String(route.br));
  const periodLabel = periods.data?.items.find((p) => p.code === per)?.label ?? per;
  const streaming = phase.kind === 'streaming';

  async function run(extra: { preset?: string; question?: string }) {
    controllerRef.current?.abort();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    controllerRef.current = controller;
    timedOut.current = false;
    setText('');
    setPhase({ kind: 'streaming' });
    const body: AiRequestBody = {
      section: route.section,
      page: route.page ?? section?.pages[0]?.id ?? 'svod',
      br: route.br,
      per,
      ...(route.section === 'finmodel' && isScenario(route.scen) ? { scen: route.scen } : {}),
      ...extra,
    };
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      timedOut.current = true;
      controller.abort();
    }, CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS);
    try {
      await streamAnalysis(
        body,
        (event) => {
          if (event.type === 'delta') setText((prev) => prev + event.text);
          else if (event.type === 'done') setPhase({ kind: 'done' });
          else if (event.type === 'error') {
            setText('');
            setPhase({ kind: 'error', message: event.message });
          }
        },
        controller.signal,
      );
      if (controller.signal.aborted) {
        setPhase(timedOut.current ? { kind: 'error', message: t('ai.error.timeout') } : { kind: 'cancelled' });
        return;
      }
      setPhase((p) => (p.kind === 'streaming' ? { kind: 'error', message: t('ai.error.interrupted') } : p));
    } catch (error) {
      if (controller.signal.aborted) {
        setPhase(timedOut.current ? { kind: 'error', message: t('ai.error.timeout') } : { kind: 'cancelled' });
      } else if (isApiError(error, 429)) {
        const key: MessageKey = error.params.scope === 'org' ? 'ai.limit.org' : 'ai.limit.user';
        setPhase({ kind: 'limit', message: t(key, { time: nextAttempt(error.retryAfter ?? 60) }) });
      } else if (isApiError(error, 404, 'ai_disabled')) {
        setPhase({ kind: 'disabled' });
      } else {
        setPhase({ kind: 'error', message: error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : t('error.generic') });
      }
    } finally {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      if (controllerRef.current === controller) controllerRef.current = null;
    }
  }

  const ask = (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const q = question.trim();
    if (q) void run({ question: q });
  };

  return (
    <Drawer open onClose={onClose} title={t('ai.title')}>
      <div className="ai">
        <p className="note">{`${section?.title ?? ''}: ${pageTitle}. ${branchName}, ${periodLabel.toLowerCase()}`}</p>
        <div className="ai-out" aria-live="polite" aria-busy={streaming}>
          {phase.kind === 'idle' ? <p>{t('ai.intro')}</p> : null}
          {streaming && text === '' ? <p className="note">{t('ai.thinking')}</p> : null}
          {text ? <AiAnswer text={text} /> : null}
          {phase.kind === 'error' || phase.kind === 'limit' ? <p className="form-error" role="alert">{phase.message}</p> : null}
          {phase.kind === 'cancelled' ? <p className="note">{t('ai.cancelled')}</p> : null}
          {phase.kind === 'disabled' ? <p className="note">{t('ai.disabled')}</p> : null}
        </div>
        <div className="ai-presets" role="group" aria-label={t('ai.presets')}>
          {PRESETS.map((p) => (
            <button key={p.id} type="button" className="ai-chip" disabled={streaming} onClick={() => void run({ preset: p.id })}>
              {t(p.key)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <form className="ai-ask" onSubmit={ask}>
          <input
            className="input"
            type="search"
            maxLength={500}
            aria-label={t('ai.question')}
            placeholder={t('ai.question')}
            value={question}
            onChange={(e) => setQuestion(e.target.value)}
          />
          <Button type="submit" variant="secondary" disabled={streaming || question.trim() === ''}>
            {t('ai.ask')}
          </Button>
        </form>
        <div className="ai-actions">
          <Button onClick={() => void run({})} disabled={streaming}>
            {t('ai.run')}
          </Button>
          {streaming ? (
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => controllerRef.current?.abort()}>
              {t('ai.cancel')}
            </Button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
        <p className="note ai-disclaimer">{t('ai.disclaimer')}</p>
      </div>
    </Drawer>
  );
}
```
(`formatDateTime` 3-reja formati `ДД.ММ.ГГГГ ЧЧ:ММ` — oxirgi 5 belgi vaqt. `flattenMenu` — `src/shell/sectionMeta.ts`.)

`frontend/src/ai/AiButton.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import type { Route } from '../router/hash';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';
import { AiDrawer } from './AiDrawer';
import { useAiStatus } from './api';

/** TZ 6/7: yuqori panel tugmasi — faqat bo'lim sahifasida va funksiya yoqilgan bo'lsa (standart — yo'q). */
export function AiButton({ menu, route }: { menu: MenuOut; route: Route }) {
  const status = useAiStatus(route.kind === 'section');
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  if (route.kind !== 'section' || !status.data?.enabled) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <button type="button" className="tool" title={t('ai.buttonTitle')} onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
        <Icon name="spark" />
        <span className="tool__label">{t('ai.button')}</span>
      </button>
      {open ? <AiDrawer menu={menu} route={route} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} /> : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import type { AiRequestOut, Page } from '../api/types';
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../i18n';
import { formatDateTime, formatNumber } from '../i18n/format';
import { Badge } from '../ui/Badge';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../ui/Card';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { PageSkeleton } from '../ui/Skeleton';
import { Table, type Column } from '../ui/Table';
import type { AdminPageProps } from './registry';

const PAGE_SIZE = 50;
const PRESET_KEYS: Record<string, MessageKey> = {
  yoy: 'ai.preset.yoy', risks: 'ai.preset.risks', priorities: 'ai.preset.priorities', plan: 'ai.preset.plan',
};
const STATUS_KEYS: Record<string, MessageKey> = {
  ok: 'aij.status.ok', running: 'aij.status.running', error: 'aij.status.error', timeout: 'aij.status.timeout',
  cancelled: 'aij.status.cancelled', refused: 'aij.status.refused',
};

/** TZ 7 «Журнал»: дата, пользователь, раздел, фильтры, вопрос, длина ответа (90 kun). `audit:read`. */
export default function AiJournalPage(_props: AdminPageProps) {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['admin', 'ai-requests', page],
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => api<Page<AiRequestOut>>('/audit/ai-requests', { signal, query: { page, page_size: PAGE_SIZE } }),
  });
  if (query.isError) return <ErrorBanner error={query.error} lastSuccessAt={query.dataUpdatedAt} onRetry={() => void query.refetch()} />;
  if (query.isPending) return <PageSkeleton />;
  const title = t('admin.page.aijournal');
  const columns: Column<AiRequestOut>[] = [
    { key: 'time', header: t('aij.col.time'), cell: (r) => formatDateTime(r.created_at) },
    { key: 'user', header: t('aij.col.user'), cell: (r) => r.username },
    { key: 'page', header: t('aij.col.page'), cell: (r) => `${r.section} / ${r.page}` },
    { key: 'question', header: t('aij.col.question'), cell: (r) => r.question ?? (r.preset ? t(PRESET_KEYS[r.preset] ?? 'ai.run') : t('ai.run')), wrap: true },
    { key: 'status', header: t('aij.col.status'), cell: (r) => <Badge tone={r.status === 'ok' ? 'ok' : r.status === 'running' ? 'blue' : 'warn'}>{t(STATUS_KEYS[r.status] ?? 'aij.status.error')}</Badge> },
    { key: 'answer', header: t('aij.col.answer'), cell: (r) => formatNumber(r.answer_chars), align: 'right' },
    { key: 'model', header: t('aij.col.model'), cell: (r) => r.model },
  ];
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(query.data.total / PAGE_SIZE));
  return (
    <Card title={title}>
      <p className="note">{t('aij.hint')}</p>
      <Table caption={title} columns={columns} rows={query.data.items} rowKey={(r) => r.id} free />
      {pages > 1 ? (
        <div className="toolbar">
          <Button variant="secondary" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p - 1)}>←</Button>
          <span className="note">{`${page} / ${pages}`}</span>
          <Button variant="secondary" disabled={page >= pages} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p + 1)}>→</Button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

### Task 16: «Экран» (kiosk) rejimi — aylanish 60 s, Esc, reduced-motion, fon so'rovlari idle'ni uzaytirmaydi, qayta kirish ekrani

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/tests/test_session_background.py`, `frontend/src/screen/store.ts`, `frontend/src/screen/useScreenRotation.ts`, `frontend/src/screen/ScreenMode.tsx`, `frontend/src/screen/ScreenEnded.tsx`, `frontend/src/screen/screen.css`
- Modify: `backend/app/auth/sessions.py` (`resolve(…, touch=True)`), `backend/app/auth/context.py` (`BACKGROUND_HEADER`, `get_ctx`), `frontend/src/shell/ShellTools.tsx` (`ScreenMode`), `frontend/src/app/queryClient.ts` (kiosk'da 401 → `markSessionEnded`), `frontend/src/app/App.tsx` (`ScreenEnded`), `frontend/src/i18n/ru.ts` (`screen.*`)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_session_background.py`, `frontend/src/screen/screen.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `sessions.resolve`, `SLIDE_EVERY`, `get_ctx`, `Header`, `tests.helpers.create_user/issue_session/use_session`, `UserSession`; frontend `setBackgroundMode`, `backgroundHeaders`, `navigate`, `Route`, `MenuOut`, `flattenMenu`, `useMediaQuery`, `Icon` (`monitor`), `Button`, `makeQueryClient`, `api`, `apiError`, `mockApi`, `MENU_FULL`, `MENU_AUDITOR`.
- Produces:
  - Backend: `app.auth.context.BACKGROUND_HEADER = "X-UGK-Background"`; `sessions.resolve(db, token, now, *, touch: bool = True)` — `touch=False` da `last_seen_at`/`idle_expires_at` o'zgarmaydi (muddati o'tgan sessiya baribir rad etiladi); `get_ctx` sarlavha `"1"` bo'lsa `touch=False`.
  - `src/screen/store.ts`: `ScreenState { active; ended }`, `startScreen()`, `stopScreen()`, `markSessionEnded()`, `dismissEnded()`, `isScreenActive()`, `getScreenState()`, `useScreenState()`; faol rejimda `<html class="screen-mode">` va `setBackgroundMode(true)`.
  - `src/screen/useScreenRotation.ts`: `WALL_ROUTE = ['obzor', 'kazna', 'proizvodstvo', 'investicii', 'finmodel', 'sklad']`, `ROTATE_MS = 60_000`, `wallSections(menu) -> string[]` (faqat menyudagilar), `useScreenRotation(menu, route) -> string[]` (har 60 s keyingi bo'lim «Сводка»si, joriy `br`/`per` bilan; Esc — chiqish).
  - `ScreenMode({ menu, route })` — «Экран» tugmasi (aylanadigan bo'lim bo'lmasa yashirin), faol rejimda portal `wallbar` («Выйти»), `html.screen-motion` faqat `prefers-reduced-motion` bo'lmasa.
  - `ScreenEnded()` — kiosk paytida sessiya tugasa to'liq ekranli «Сеанс завершён» oynasi, «Войти снова».

- [ ] **Step 1: Testlarni yozish (backend)**

`backend/tests/test_session_background.py`:
```python
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.main import create_app
from tests.helpers import create_user, issue_session, use_session


def _times(session_id: object) -> tuple[datetime, datetime]:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        row = conn.execute(text("SELECT last_seen_at, idle_expires_at FROM core.sessions "
                                "WHERE id = :i"), {"i": session_id}).one()
    return row.last_seen_at, row.idle_expires_at


def _age(session_id: object, *, seen_ago: timedelta, idle_left: timedelta) -> None:
    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    with get_engine("app").begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.sessions SET last_seen_at = :s, idle_expires_at = :e "
                          "WHERE id = :i"),
                     {"s": now - seen_ago, "e": now + idle_left, "i": session_id})


def test_background_requests_do_not_extend_idle_timeout() -> None:
    user = create_user("rukovodstvo")
    token, csrf, session_id = issue_session(user.id)
    with TestClient(create_app(), base_url="https://testserver") as client:
        use_session(client, token, csrf)
        _age(session_id, seen_ago=timedelta(minutes=5), idle_left=timedelta(minutes=25))
        before = _times(session_id)
        r = client.get("/api/v1/auth/me", headers={"X-UGK-Background": "1"})
        assert r.status_code == 200
        assert _times(session_id) == before  # kiosk aylanishi idle'ni uzaytirmaydi
        assert client.get("/api/v1/auth/me").status_code == 200
        seen, idle = _times(session_id)
        assert seen > before[0] and idle > before[1]  # inson harakati — uzaytiradi


def test_expired_session_is_rejected_even_in_background() -> None:
    user = create_user("rukovodstvo")
    token, csrf, session_id = issue_session(user.id)
    with TestClient(create_app(), base_url="https://testserver") as client:
        use_session(client, token, csrf)
        _age(session_id, seen_ago=timedelta(minutes=31), idle_left=timedelta(seconds=-1))
        r = client.get("/api/v1/auth/me", headers={"X-UGK-Background": "1"})
        assert r.status_code == 401 and r.json()["error"]["code"] == "session_expired"
```

`frontend/src/screen/screen.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { api, backgroundHeaders } from '../api/client';
import { makeQueryClient } from '../app/queryClient';
import type { Route } from '../router/hash';
import { MENU_AUDITOR, MENU_FULL } from '../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, mockApi } from '../test/mockApi';
import { ScreenEnded } from './ScreenEnded';
import { ScreenMode } from './ScreenMode';
import { dismissEnded, getScreenState, startScreen, stopScreen } from './store';
import { ROTATE_MS, wallSections } from './useScreenRotation';

const route: Route = { kind: 'section', section: 'kredity', page: 'reestr', br: 3, per: 'm08' };

function stubReducedMotion(reduce: boolean) {
  vi.spyOn(window, 'matchMedia').mockImplementation((query: string) => ({
    matches: reduce && query.includes('prefers-reduced-motion'),
    media: query, onchange: null,
    addEventListener: () => undefined, removeEventListener: () => undefined,
    addListener: () => undefined, removeListener: () => undefined, dispatchEvent: () => false,
  }));
}

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
  window.history.replaceState(null, '', '#kredity/reestr?br=3&per=m08');
});

afterEach(() => {
  act(() => stopScreen());
  dismissEnded();
  vi.useRealTimers();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('Экран (kiosk)', () => {
  it('lists only permitted sections in the TZ order', () => {
    expect(wallSections(MENU_FULL)).toEqual(['obzor', 'kazna', 'proizvodstvo', 'investicii', 'finmodel', 'sklad']);
    expect(wallSections(MENU_AUDITOR)).toEqual(['obzor']);
  });

  it('rotates every 60 seconds keeping filters, marks requests as background and exits on Esc', () => {
    stubReducedMotion(false);
    render(<ScreenMode menu={MENU_FULL} route={route} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Экран' }));
    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveClass('screen-mode');
    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveClass('screen-motion');
    expect(backgroundHeaders()).toEqual({ 'X-UGK-Background': '1' });
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod?br=3&per=m08');
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(ROTATE_MS));
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#kazna/svod?br=3&per=m08');
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(ROTATE_MS * 5));
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod?br=3&per=m08');
    expect(screen.getByRole('status')).toHaveTextContent('Автопоказ: раздел меняется каждую минуту');
    fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: 'Escape' });
    expect(document.documentElement).not.toHaveClass('screen-mode');
    expect(backgroundHeaders()).toEqual({});
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(ROTATE_MS));
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod?br=3&per=m08');
  });

  it('respects prefers-reduced-motion and exits with the wallbar button', () => {
    stubReducedMotion(true);
    render(<ScreenMode menu={MENU_AUDITOR} route={route} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Экран' }));
    expect(document.documentElement).toHaveClass('screen-mode');
    expect(document.documentElement).not.toHaveClass('screen-motion');
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(ROTATE_MS));
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#obzor/svod?br=3&per=m08');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Выйти' }));
    expect(getScreenState().active).toBe(false);
  });

  it('shows a clear re-login screen when the session ends in kiosk mode', async () => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/svod': apiError(401, 'session_expired', 'Сеанс завершён, войдите снова') });
    const client = makeQueryClient(() => undefined);
    act(() => startScreen());
    await act(async () => {
      await client.fetchQuery({ queryKey: ['section', '/obzor/svod'], queryFn: () => api('/obzor/svod') }).catch(() => undefined);
    });
    expect(getScreenState()).toEqual({ active: false, ended: true });
    expect(window.location.hash.startsWith('#login')).toBe(true);
    render(<ScreenEnded />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('alertdialog', { name: 'Сеанс завершён' })).toHaveTextContent('30 минут');
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Войти снова' }));
    expect(screen.queryByRole('alertdialog')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/screen/store.ts`:
```ts
import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { setBackgroundMode } from '../api/client';

export interface ScreenState {
  active: boolean;
  ended: boolean;
}

let state: ScreenState = { active: false, ended: false };
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

function apply(next: ScreenState): void {
  state = next;
  document.documentElement.classList.toggle('screen-mode', next.active);
  setBackgroundMode(next.active);
  listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
}

function subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

export function startScreen(): void {
  apply({ active: true, ended: false });
}

export function stopScreen(): void {
  if (state.active) apply({ ...state, active: false });
}

/** Kiosk paytida sessiya tugadi (401) — rejim to'xtaydi, aniq qayta kirish ekrani ko'rsatiladi. */
export function markSessionEnded(): void {
  apply({ active: false, ended: true });
}

export function dismissEnded(): void {
  if (state.ended) apply({ ...state, ended: false });
}

export function isScreenActive(): boolean {
  return state.active;
}

export function getScreenState(): ScreenState {
  return state;
}

export function useScreenState(): ScreenState {
  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getScreenState, getScreenState);
}
```

`frontend/src/screen/useScreenRotation.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import type { MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import type { Route } from '../router/hash';
import { navigate } from '../router/store';
import { flattenMenu } from '../shell/sectionMeta';
import { stopScreen, useScreenState } from './store';

/** TZ 6: Обзор → Казначейство → Производство → Инвестиции → Финмодель → Склад, har 60 soniyada. */
export const WALL_ROUTE = ['obzor', 'kazna', 'proizvodstvo', 'investicii', 'finmodel', 'sklad'] as const;
export const ROTATE_MS = 60_000;

export function wallSections(menu: MenuOut): string[] {
  const allowed = new Set(flattenMenu(menu).map((s) => s.id));
  return WALL_ROUTE.filter((id) => allowed.has(id));
}

export function useScreenRotation(menu: MenuOut, route: Route): string[] {
  const { active } = useScreenState();
  const sections = useMemo(() => wallSections(menu), [menu]);
  const routeRef = useRef(route);
  routeRef.current = route;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active || sections.length === 0) return undefined;
    let index = 0;
    const show = () => {
      const current = routeRef.current;
      navigate({
        kind: 'section',
        section: sections[index % sections.length] ?? 'obzor',
        page: 'svod',
        br: current.kind === 'section' ? current.br : null,
        per: current.kind === 'section' ? current.per : null,
      });
    };
    show();
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      index += 1;
      show();
    }, ROTATE_MS);
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') stopScreen();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => {
      window.clearInterval(timer);
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    };
  }, [active, sections]);

  return sections;
}
```
(`routeRef` navigatsiyadan keyin yangi marshrutni ushlaydi; kiosk yangi `br`/`per` ni qabul qilmaydi — ular foydalanuvchi boshlagan paytdagi filtrlar bo'lib qoladi, chunki aylanish ularni o'zi o'zgartirmaydi.)

- [ ] **Step 6: Implementatsiya — komponentlar, 401 va uslublar**

`frontend/src/screen/ScreenMode.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import type { MenuOut } from '../api/types';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import type { Route } from '../router/hash';
import { useMediaQuery } from '../shell/useMediaQuery';
import { Icon } from '../ui/icons';
import './screen.css';
import { startScreen, stopScreen, useScreenState } from './store';
import { useScreenRotation } from './useScreenRotation';

/** TZ 6 «Режим экрана»: tugma, aylanish, pastki «wallbar» (asboblar yashirin bo'lgani uchun portal). */
export function ScreenMode({ menu, route }: { menu: MenuOut; route: Route }) {
  const { active } = useScreenState();
  const sections = useScreenRotation(menu, route);
  const reduced = useMediaQuery('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle('screen-motion', active && !reduced);
    return () => document.documentElement.classList.remove('screen-motion');
  }, [active, reduced]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (active && route.kind !== 'section') stopScreen();
  }, [active, route.kind]);

  if (sections.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <button type="button" className="tool" title={t('screen.buttonTitle')} onClick={startScreen}>
        <Icon name="monitor" />
        <span className="tool__label">{t('screen.button')}</span>
      </button>
      {active
        ? createPortal(
            <div className="wallbar" role="status">
              <span>{t('screen.info')}</span>
              <button type="button" className="wallbar__exit" onClick={stopScreen}>
                {t('screen.exit')}
              </button>
            </div>,
            document.body,
          )
        : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/screen/ScreenEnded.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { t } from '../i18n';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
import './screen.css';
import { dismissEnded, useScreenState } from './store';

/** Kiosk paytida sessiya tugasa — jim login sahifasi o'rniga aniq tushuntirish (TZ 6 holatlari). */
export function ScreenEnded() {
  const { ended } = useScreenState();
  const titleId = useId();
  if (!ended) return null;
  return createPortal(
    <div className="screen-ended" role="alertdialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby={titleId}>
      <div className="screen-ended__card">
        <h2 id={titleId}>{t('screen.ended.title')}</h2>
        <p>{t('screen.ended.text')}</p>
        <Button onClick={dismissEnded}>{t('screen.ended.login')}</Button>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}
```
(Login sahifasi `onUnauthenticated` tomonidan allaqachon ochilgan — tugma oynani yopadi va login formasi ko'rinadi.)

```css
/* S5 «Экран» (prototip body.wall): menyu, filtrlar, chiplar va asboblar yashirin, shrift katta. */
html.screen-mode { font-size: clamp(20px, 1.6vw, 30px); }
html.screen-mode .side,
html.screen-mode .filters,
html.screen-mode .chips,
html.screen-mode .tools,
html.screen-mode .head__menu,
html.screen-mode .skip { display: none !important; }
html.screen-mode .layout { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); }
.wallbar { position: fixed; right: 1rem; bottom: 1rem; z-index: 40; display: flex; gap: 0.6rem; align-items: center; padding: 0.4rem 0.5rem 0.4rem 1rem; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 999px; background: var(--card); color: var(--ink); font-size: 0.9rem; }
.wallbar__exit { min-height: 44px; padding: 0 1rem; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 999px; background: var(--soft); color: var(--brand); cursor: pointer; }
.screen-ended { position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 100; display: grid; place-items: center; padding: 1rem; background: var(--bg); }
.screen-ended__card { max-width: 34rem; padding: 2rem; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 1rem; background: var(--card); text-align: center; }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
  html.screen-motion main { animation: screen-in 0.4s ease-out; }
}
@keyframes screen-in { from { opacity: 0.2; } to { opacity: 1; } }
@media print { .wallbar { display: none; } }
```

`frontend/e2e/specs/finmodel-ai-screen.spec.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { adminFor, expectNoHorizontalScroll, isMobile, signIn } from '../fixtures';

function watchCsp(page: Page): string[] {
  const violations: string[] = [];
  page.on('console', (msg) => {
    const text = msg.text();
    if (/Content Security Policy|Refused to/i.test(text)) violations.push(text);
  });
  return violations;
}

async function csrf(page: Page): Promise<string> {
  const cookie = (await page.context().cookies()).find((c) => c.name === '__Host-ugk_csrf');
  if (!cookie) throw new Error('CSRF cookie topilmadi');
  return cookie.value;
}

async function setAi(page: Page, value: boolean): Promise<void> {
  const r = await page.request.put('/api/v1/admin/settings/ai.enabled', { data: { value }, headers: { 'X-CSRF-Token': await csrf(page) } });
  expect(r.status()).toBe(200);
}

test('Финмодель: сводка, сценарий в адресе, доходы, долг, сценарии', async ({ page }, info) => {
  const csp = watchCsp(page);
  await page.goto('/#finmodel/svod');
  await signIn(page, adminFor(info.project.name));
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Финмодель: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Мощность к 2032 году')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('img', { name: 'Выручка и EBITDA по годам' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Сценарий модели' }).getByRole('button', { name: 'Маловодье' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#finmodel\/svod\?scen=water$/);
  await expect(page.getByText('Выработка ниже нормы на 15% в 2027–2029 годах')).toBeVisible();
  if (!isMobile(info.project.name)) {
    // Chap menyu havolasi ssenariyni bo'lim ichida saqlaydi (mobil'da menyu — yopiq Drawer).
    await page.getByRole('link', { name: /Доходы и расходы/ }).first().click();
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#finmodel\/dohody\?scen=water$/);
  } else {
    await page.goto('/#finmodel/dohody?scen=water');
  }
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Себестоимость и тариф' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.goto('/#finmodel/dolg');
  await expect(page.getByRole('img', { name: 'Покрытие долга (DSCR)' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.goto('/#finmodel/scenarii');
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Сравнение сценариев' }).locator('tbody tr')).toHaveCount(4);
  await expectNoHorizontalScroll(page);
  expect(csp).toEqual([]);
});

test('Финмодель: экономист филиала не имеет доступа, DSCR в «Кредитах» — из модели', async ({ page }, info) => {
  test.skip(info.project.name !== 'desktop-1440', 'bitta kenglik yetarli');
  await page.goto('/#obzor/svod');
  await signIn(page, 'e2e_branch');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Обзор: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
  expect((await page.request.get('/api/v1/finmodel/model')).status()).toBe(403);
  await page.context().clearCookies();
  await page.goto('/#kredity/svod');
  await signIn(page, adminFor(info.project.name));
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Покрытие обслуживания долга (DSCR)' })).toBeVisible();
  const svod = await (await page.request.get('/api/v1/kredity/svod')).json() as { kpis: { dscr: number | null } };
  expect(svod.kpis.dscr).not.toBeNull();
});

test('AI-анализ: выключен по умолчанию, после включения — потоковый ответ из трёх блоков', async ({ page }, info) => {
  test.skip(info.project.name !== 'desktop-1440', 'global sozlama — bitta loyihada');
  const csp = watchCsp(page);
  await page.goto('/#kredity/svod');
  await signIn(page, adminFor(info.project.name));
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Кредиты: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'AI-анализ' })).toHaveCount(0);
  const r = await page.request.post('/api/v1/ai/analyze', { data: { section: 'kredity', page: 'svod' }, headers: { 'X-CSRF-Token': await csrf(page) } });
  expect(r.status()).toBe(404);
  await setAi(page, true);
  try {
    await page.reload();
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'AI-анализ' }).click();
    const drawer = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'AI-анализ' });
    await drawer.getByRole('button', { name: 'Проанализировать' }).click();
    await expect(drawer.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Что важно заметить' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(drawer.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Действия на ближайшие две недели' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(drawer.getByText('Ответ носит рекомендательный характер и не заменяет решение ответственного лица.')).toBeVisible();
    await expect(drawer.getByRole('button', { name: 'Отменить' })).toHaveCount(0);
    await drawer.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Свой вопрос по данным страницы' }).fill('Проверь ИНН 301234567');
    await drawer.getByRole('button', { name: 'Спросить' }).click();
    await expect(drawer.getByRole('alert')).toContainText('Вопрос содержит реквизиты');
    const journal = await (await page.request.get('/api/v1/audit/ai-requests')).json() as { items: Array<{ section: string; status: string }> };
    expect(journal.items[0]).toMatchObject({ section: 'kredity', status: 'ok' });
  } finally {
    await setAi(page, false);
  }
  expect(csp).toEqual([]);
});

test('Экран: скрытое меню, смена раздела через минуту, выход по Esc', async ({ page }, info) => {
  test.skip(info.project.name === 'mobile-390', 'devor ekrani — keng ekranlar uchun');
  await page.clock.install();
  await page.goto('/#kazna/svod');
  await signIn(page, adminFor(info.project.name));
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Казначейство: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Экран' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Обзор: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator('.filters')).toBeHidden();
  await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'Автопоказ' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.clock.fastForward('01:00');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Казначейство: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  await expect(page.locator('.filters')).toBeVisible();
  await page.clock.fastForward('01:00');
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Казначейство: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
});
```
(Sarlavhalar — 3-reja `SectionScreen`: «Сводка» uchun `«<bo'lim>: сводка»`. AI testi global `ai.enabled` sozlamasini vaqtincha yoqadi — shuning uchun faqat bitta loyihada; `finally` uni o'chiradi. Dev stack'da `AI_PROVIDER=fake` — tarmoqqa chiqish yo'q.)
